# aiTrends 2D Studio

Turn any voiceover into a fully animated, cinematic stick-figure video: black stick figures with glowing rim light acting out the narration in moody 2.5D sets, giant face close-ups, freeze-frame "time stopped" moments, animated numbers and charts, blueprint-style explainers and low-poly 3D orbit shots, with generated music and sound effects.

Every frame is drawn by code and rendered frame by frame. No image or video AI is used, so characters stay consistent and text stays sharp. Visuals are invented for each script; the style stays the same.

## What you can say
- "Make a 2D video from this voiceover": runs the whole pipeline.
- `/new-video`: set up a video, timestamp the narration, plan the shots.
- `/video-styles`: see your own shots in 5 looks (Cinematic Dusk, Midnight Blue, Golden Hour, Neon Noir, Soft Storybook), pick one or design your own.
- `/render-video`: build and check the scenes, preview, render the final video.
- `/video-sound`: music moods, sound effects, ambience, final mix.

## Inputs and options
- **Voiceover** (any common audio format).
- **Script:** optional. Timestamped (SRT/VTT/JSON/TXT) or plain (timed automatically). Without a script, the voiceover is transcribed.
- **16:9 or 9:16**, **720p / 1080p / 1440p / 4K**, optional burned-in captions.
- **Music:** generated by mood, your own track, or none. **SFX:** 36 built-in effects, or your own files.

## First-time setup (automatic)
On first use, Claude copies the bundled studio into your project folder (`aitrends-2d-studio/`) and installs:
- ffmpeg,
- Node.js packages (renderer, Three.js, fonts),
- a headless Chromium,
- Python's numpy, scipy and faster-whisper.

Whisper downloads its speech model on the first transcription. Roughly 1 GB of disk is needed in total.

## Render times
A small 2-core cloud machine renders 720p at about 2 frames per second (a 47-second video takes ~11 minutes). 1080p takes about 2× longer and 4K about 8×. A modern desktop is 3–5× faster. Long videos at 4K are best rendered overnight.

## Contents
- `skills/make-2d-video/`: the full pipeline, style bible and references, plus the studio kit (`kit/`: renderer, animation engine, transcription tools, sound synthesis and a reference example project).
- `skills/new-video`, `skills/video-styles`, `skills/render-video`, `skills/video-sound`: stage commands.

No API keys or connectors are required.

"""audio/dsp.py — tiny synthesis/DSP toolkit (numpy + scipy). 48 kHz stereo float32 everywhere."""
import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

SR = 48000


def secs(n): return int(round(n * SR))
def t_axis(n): return np.arange(n) / SR
def rng(seed): return np.random.default_rng(seed)
def db(x): return 10 ** (x / 20)


def noise(n, seed=0, color="white"):
    x = rng(seed).standard_normal(n).astype(np.float32)
    if color == "pink":
        b, a = [0.049922035, -0.095993537, 0.050612699, -0.004408786], [1, -2.494956002, 2.017265875, -0.522189400]
        x = signal.lfilter(b, a, x).astype(np.float32) * 4
    elif color == "brown":
        x = np.cumsum(x).astype(np.float32); x = signal.sosfilt(signal.butter(1, 25, "highpass", fs=SR, output="sos"), x).astype(np.float32); x = x / (np.abs(x).max() + 1e-9)
    return x


def _sos(kind, f, order=2):
    f = np.clip(np.atleast_1d(f), 10, SR / 2 - 100)
    return signal.butter(order, f if len(f) > 1 else f[0], btype=kind, fs=SR, output="sos")


def lp(x, f, order=2): return signal.sosfilt(_sos("lowpass", f, order), x, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
def hp(x, f, order=2): return signal.sosfilt(_sos("highpass", f, order), x, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
def bp(x, lo, hi, order=2): return signal.sosfilt(_sos("bandpass", [lo, hi], order), x, axis=0).astype(np.float32)


def sweep_filter(x, f0, f1, q=1.2, kind="bp", curve=1.0):
    """time-varying state-variable filter (cutoff glides f0 -> f1). Works block-wise for speed."""
    n = len(x); out = np.zeros_like(x); blk = 256
    low = band = 0.0
    for i in range(0, n, blk):
        u = (i / max(1, n - 1)) ** curve
        fc = f0 * (f1 / f0) ** u
        f = 2 * np.sin(np.pi * min(fc, SR / 6) / SR); damp = 1 / q
        seg = x[i:i + blk]; o = np.empty_like(seg)
        for k, s in enumerate(seg):
            low += f * band; high = s - low - damp * band; band += f * high
            o[k] = band if kind == "bp" else low if kind == "lp" else high
        out[i:i + blk] = o
    return out


def env_adsr(n, a=0.01, d=0.1, s=0.0, r=0.1, curve=4.0):
    t = t_axis(n); e = np.zeros(n, np.float32)
    A, D = a, a + d
    e[t < A] = (t[t < A] / max(a, 1e-6))
    m = (t >= A) & (t < D); e[m] = 1 - (1 - s) * (1 - np.exp(-curve * (t[m] - A) / max(d, 1e-6))) / (1 - np.exp(-curve))
    rel_start = n / SR - r
    m2 = t >= D; e[m2] = s
    m3 = t >= rel_start; e[m3] = e[m3] * np.clip((n / SR - t[m3]) / max(r, 1e-6), 0, 1)
    return e


def expdec(n, tau): return np.exp(-t_axis(n) / tau).astype(np.float32)


def sine(freq, n, phase=0.0):
    f = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(freq, dtype=np.float64), (n,))
    ph = 2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR + phase
    return np.sin(ph).astype(np.float32)


_tables = {}
def wavetable(kind, harmonics):
    key = (kind, harmonics)
    if key not in _tables:
        N = 4096; ph = np.arange(N) / N; w = np.zeros(N)
        for h in range(1, harmonics + 1):
            if kind == "saw": w += np.sin(2 * np.pi * h * ph) / h
            elif kind == "square" and h % 2: w += np.sin(2 * np.pi * h * ph) / h
            elif kind == "tri" and h % 2: w += ((-1) ** ((h - 1) // 2)) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * h * ph) / h ** 2
        _tables[key] = (w / np.abs(w).max()).astype(np.float32)
    return _tables[key]


def osc(kind, freq, n, phase=0.0):
    if kind == "sine": return sine(freq, n, phase)
    fmax = float(np.max(freq))
    harm = max(1, min(60, int((SR / 2 * 0.9) / max(fmax, 20))))
    tab = wavetable(kind, harm)
    f = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(freq, dtype=np.float64), (n,))
    ph = (np.cumsum(f) / SR + phase / (2 * np.pi)) % 1.0
    return tab[(ph * len(tab)).astype(np.int64) % len(tab)]


def midi_hz(m): return 440.0 * 2 ** ((m - 69) / 12)


def stereo(x, pan=0.0, width=0.0, seed=0):
    """mono -> stereo with constant-power pan and optional decorrelated width (short delay)."""
    l = np.cos((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4); r = np.sin((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4)
    if width > 0:
        d = int(SR * 0.011 * width)
        xr = np.concatenate([np.zeros(d, np.float32), x[:-d]]) if d > 0 else x
        return np.stack([x * l * np.sqrt(2), (x * (1 - width) + xr * width) * r * np.sqrt(2)], axis=1).astype(np.float32)
    return np.stack([x * l * np.sqrt(2), x * r * np.sqrt(2)], axis=1).astype(np.float32)


def as_stereo(x): return x if x.ndim == 2 else stereo(x)


_irs = {}
def reverb(x, size=2.0, wet=0.25, seed=7, damp=5000):
    key = (size, seed, damp)
    if key not in _irs:
        n = secs(size); r = rng(seed)
        ir = r.standard_normal((n, 2)).astype(np.float32) * np.exp(-np.arange(n) / SR * 6.9 / size)[:, None]
        ir = lp(ir, damp); ir[: secs(0.012)] *= np.linspace(0, 1, secs(0.012))[:, None]
        _irs[key] = ir / np.sqrt((ir ** 2).sum(axis=0, keepdims=True)) * 0.5
    ir = _irs[key]; x = as_stereo(x)
    y = np.stack([signal.oaconvolve(x[:, c], ir[:, c])[: len(x)] for c in range(2)], axis=1)
    return (x * (1 - wet) + y * wet * 2).astype(np.float32)


def delay(x, time=0.3, fb=0.35, wet=0.3, pingpong=True):
    x = as_stereo(x); d = secs(time); x = np.concatenate([x, np.zeros((d * 6, 2), np.float32)]); y = x.copy(); tap = x.copy()
    for k in range(8):
        tap = np.concatenate([np.zeros((d, 2), np.float32), tap[:-d]]) * (fb if k else 1.0)
        if pingpong: tap = tap[:, ::-1]
        y += tap * wet
        if np.abs(tap).max() < 1e-4: break
    return y.astype(np.float32)


def fade(x, fin=0.005, fout=0.02):
    x = x.copy(); a, b = secs(fin), secs(fout)
    if a: x[:a] *= np.linspace(0, 1, a)[:, None] if x.ndim == 2 else np.linspace(0, 1, a)
    if b: x[-b:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, b)[:, None] if x.ndim == 2 else np.linspace(1, 0, b)
    return x


def normalize(x, peak_db=-1.0):
    m = np.abs(x).max(); return (x / m * db(peak_db)).astype(np.float32) if m > 0 else x


def rms_db(x):
    v = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(x, dtype=np.float64))); return 20 * np.log10(v + 1e-12)


def place(dst, src, at, gain=1.0):
    """add src into dst starting at sample `at` (clipped to bounds)."""
    if at >= len(dst) or at + len(src) <= 0: return
    s0 = max(0, -at); d0 = max(0, at); n = min(len(src) - s0, len(dst) - d0)
    dst[d0:d0 + n] += src[s0:s0 + n] * gain


def write_wav(path, x):
    from scipy.io import wavfile
    x = np.clip(as_stereo(x), -1, 1)
    wavfile.write(path, SR, (x * 32767).astype(np.int16))

FILE: skills/make-2d-video/kit/audio/mix.py

"""audio/mix.py — build the soundtrack (voice + music + SFX + ambience), duck, master, and mux.

  python audio/mix.py --project projects/my-video [--video build/video_silent.mp4] [--no-music] [--no-sfx]

Reads config.json and story/shots.json:
  shot "sfx":  [{"at": 0.2, "name": "whoosh", "gain": -8, "dur": 0.7, "seed": 3, "pitch": 1.0}]  (at = seconds from shot start;
               name may also be a path to your own .wav/.mp3 relative to the project)
  shot "amb":  {"name": "rain", "gain": -20}   ambience bed looped under that shot (0.4 s fades)
  story "music": plan for audio/music.py  (or config.music.file = your own track, looped/trimmed)
Ducking: music −9 dB and ambience −5 dB while the narrator speaks. Master: loudness-normalised to config.loudness (default −14 LUFS).
Outputs build/mix.wav and, if the silent video exists, out/<title>.mp4.
"""
import argparse, json, os, re, subprocess, sys
import numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "tools"))
from dsp import SR, secs, db, rms_db, place, write_wav, as_stereo, lp
from common import load_audio
import sfx as SFX
import music as MUSIC


def envelope(x, att=0.03, rel=0.45):
    """smoothed voice level 0..1 used to duck the music."""
    mono = np.abs(x).mean(axis=1) if x.ndim == 2 else np.abs(x)
    hop = secs(0.01); frames = np.sqrt(np.add.reduceat(mono ** 2, np.arange(0, len(mono), hop)) / hop)
    lv = np.clip((20 * np.log10(frames + 1e-9) + 50) / 30, 0, 1)
    out = np.zeros_like(lv); a, r = np.exp(-0.01 / att), np.exp(-0.01 / rel); v = 0.0
    for i, s in enumerate(lv):
        v = a * v + (1 - a) * s if s > v else r * v + (1 - r) * s; out[i] = v
    return np.repeat(out, hop)[: len(mono)]


def load_any(path, sr=SR):
    return load_audio(path, sr, mono=False)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--project", required=True); ap.add_argument("--video"); ap.add_argument("--no-music", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--no-sfx", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--out")
    a = ap.parse_args(); P = a.project
    cfg = json.load(open(os.path.join(P, "config.json"), encoding="utf-8")); story = json.load(open(os.path.join(P, "story", "shots.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    total = story.get("duration") or story["shots"][-1]["end"]; n = secs(total)
    voice = load_any(os.path.join(P, cfg["voiceover"]))
    voice = np.concatenate([voice, np.zeros((max(0, n - len(voice)), 2), np.float32)])[:n]
    v_rms = rms_db(voice[np.abs(voice).mean(1) > db(-40)]) if np.any(np.abs(voice).mean(1) > db(-40)) else -20
    env = envelope(voice)
    mix = voice.copy()
    # ---- music
    mcfg = cfg.get("music", {}) or {}
    if mcfg.get("enabled", True) and not a.no_music:
        if mcfg.get("file"):
            mus = load_any(os.path.join(P, mcfg["file"]))
            reps = int(np.ceil(n / len(mus))); mus = np.concatenate([mus] * reps)[:n]
            k = secs(3); mus[-k:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, k)[:, None]
        else:
            path = os.path.join(P, "build", "music.wav")
            plan = story.get("music") or MUSIC.default_plan(total)
            print(f"generating music: {len(plan)} section(s) — " + ", ".join(f"{s['mood']}@{s.get('intensity', .5)}" for s in plan))
            mus = MUSIC.render_score(plan, total, mcfg.get("seed", 1), mcfg.get("key")); write_wav(path, mus)
        mus = as_stereo(mus)[:n]; mus = np.concatenate([mus, np.zeros((n - len(mus), 2), np.float32)])
        mus *= db(v_rms + mcfg.get("gainDb", -17) - rms_db(mus))
        mus *= db(-9 * env)[:, None]
        mix += mus
    # ---- sfx + ambience
    scfg = cfg.get("sfx", {}) or {}
    if scfg.get("enabled", True) and not a.no_sfx:
        bed = np.zeros((n, 2), np.float32); hits = np.zeros((n, 2), np.float32); cache = {}
        cues = []
        for s in story["shots"]:
            for c in s.get("sfx", []) or []: cues.append({**c, "t": s["start"] + float(c.get("at", 0))})
            if s.get("amb"):
                am = s["amb"]; ln = s["end"] - s["start"]
                key = (am["name"], am.get("seed", 1)); base = cache.get(key)
                if base is None: base = cache[key] = SFX.make(am["name"], dur=6.0, seed=am.get("seed", 1))
                loop = np.concatenate([base] * int(np.ceil(ln / (len(base) / SR) + 1)))[: secs(ln)]
                f = min(secs(0.4), len(loop) // 2); loop[:f] *= np.linspace(0, 1, f)[:, None]; loop[-f:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, f)[:, None]
                place(bed, loop, secs(s["start"]), db(am.get("gain", -20)))
        for c in story.get("sfx", []) or []: cues.append({**c, "t": float(c["t"])})
        for c in cues:
            nm = c["name"]
            if re.search(r"\.(wav|mp3|ogg|flac|m4a)$", nm, re.I): snd = load_any(os.path.join(P, nm))
            else: snd = SFX.make(nm, dur=c.get("dur"), seed=c.get("seed"), pitch=c.get("pitch"))
            place(hits, snd, secs(c["t"]), db(c.get("gain", -8)))
        bed *= db(-5 * env)[:, None]
        g = db(scfg.get("gainDb", -6) + (v_rms + 18))   # SFX levels are relative to the voice level
        mix += (hits + bed) * g
        print(f"sfx: {len(cues)} cue(s), ambience on {sum(1 for s in story['shots'] if s.get('amb'))} shot(s)")
    # ---- master: gentle high-pass, soft clip, loudness normalise with ffmpeg
    mix = mix - lp(mix, 25)
    peak = np.abs(mix).max()
    if peak > 0.95: mix = np.tanh(mix / peak * 1.2) / np.tanh(1.2) * 0.95
    raw = os.path.join(P, "build", "mix_raw.wav"); write_wav(raw, mix)
    out_wav = os.path.join(P, "build", "mix.wav"); lufs = cfg.get("loudness", -14)
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", raw, "-af", f"loudnorm=I={lufs}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11", "-ar", str(SR), out_wav], check=True)
    print(f"wrote {out_wav}  ({total:.1f}s, target {lufs} LUFS)")
    video = a.video or os.path.join(P, "build", "video_silent.mp4")
    if os.path.exists(video):
        safe = re.sub(r"[^\w\- ]+", "", cfg.get("title") or "video").strip().replace(" ", "_") or "video"
        out = a.out or os.path.join(P, "out", f"{safe}.mp4"); os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", video, "-i", out_wav, "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0", "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-shortest", "-movflags", "+faststart", out], check=True)
        print(f"FINAL VIDEO: {out}")
    else:
        print(f"(no silent video at {video} yet — render it, then run this again to mux)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: skills/make-2d-video/kit/audio/music.py

SHA256: a24ae17616b9255e79e0ae541427910261f5bd661b574fbfcba4829df3a7c7d2

"""audio/music.py — procedural, mood-driven background score (royalty-free, generated locally).

  python audio/music.py --project projects/my-video            # reads story/shots.json "music" plan
  python audio/music.py --mood tension --dur 30 --out test.wav  # audition one mood
  python audio/music.py --list

Plan format (story/shots.json -> "music"): contiguous sections in seconds
  [{"start": 0, "end": 42, "mood": "mystery", "intensity": 0.4},
   {"start": 42, "end": 95, "mood": "tension", "intensity": 0.7}, ...]
Moods: mystery, tension, action, wonder, hopeful, sad, triumph, playful, calm, dark, epic
intensity 0..1 adds layers (pulse, arps, drums) and opens the filter. Sections cross-fade over ~2 s.
"""
import argparse, json, os, sys
import numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
from dsp import SR, secs, t_axis, noise, lp, hp, bp, osc, sine, expdec, env_adsr, stereo, reverb, delay, midi_hz, rng, write_wav, rms_db, db, place

NOTE = {"C": 0, "C#": 1, "Db": 1, "D": 2, "D#": 3, "Eb": 3, "E": 4, "F": 5, "F#": 6, "Gb": 6, "G": 7, "G#": 8, "Ab": 8, "A": 9, "A#": 10, "Bb": 10, "B": 11}
MOODS = {
    "mystery":  dict(key="D", minor=True,  prog=["i", "bVI", "bVII", "i"], bpm=72, bars=2, bright=0.35, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, bell=0.0, pulse=0.35, tick=0.55, arp=0.75)),
    "tension":  dict(key="C#", minor=True, prog=["i", "i", "bVI", "V"], bpm=96, bars=1, bright=0.45, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, pulse=0.0, tick=0.3, kick=0.6, drone=0.0)),
    "action":   dict(key="E", minor=True,  prog=["i", "bVII", "bVI", "bVII"], bpm=128, bars=1, bright=0.6, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, pulse=0, kick=0.3, snare=0.45, hat=0.35, brass=0.7)),
    "wonder":   dict(key="F", minor=False, prog=["I", "V", "vi", "IV"], bpm=80, bars=2, bright=0.6, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0.2, arp=0.0, bell=0.3, piano=0.5)),
    "hopeful":  dict(key="G", minor=False, prog=["IV", "I", "V", "vi"], bpm=88, bars=1, bright=0.55, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, piano=0, arp=0.5, kick=0.7, hat=0.75)),
    "sad":      dict(key="A", minor=True,  prog=["i", "bVI", "bIII", "bVII"], bpm=66, bars=2, bright=0.3, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0.2, piano=0, bell=0.6)),
    "triumph":  dict(key="D", minor=False, prog=["I", "V", "vi", "IV"], bpm=108, bars=1, bright=0.65, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, arp=0.2, kick=0.35, snare=0.5, hat=0.45, brass=0.3)),
    "playful":  dict(key="C", minor=False, prog=["I", "vi", "ii", "V"], bpm=112, bars=1, bright=0.6, layers=dict(pluck=0, bass=0, hat=0.3, clap=0.4, bell=0.6)),
    "calm":     dict(key="Eb", minor=False, prog=["I", "IV", "vi", "V"], bpm=70, bars=2, bright=0.4, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0.2, piano=0.2)),
    "dark":     dict(key="B", minor=True,  prog=["i", "bII", "i", "bVI"], bpm=60, bars=2, bright=0.25, layers=dict(drone=0, pad=0.2, sub=0, bell=0.4, tick=0.6)),
    "epic":     dict(key="C", minor=True,  prog=["i", "bVI", "bIII", "bVII"], bpm=100, bars=1, bright=0.6, layers=dict(pad=0, sub=0, pulse=0, kick=0.3, snare=0.55, brass=0.2, arp=0.6)),
}
ROMAN = {"I": 0, "II": 2, "III": 4, "IV": 5, "V": 7, "VI": 9, "VII": 11}


def chord_tones(rn, key_midi):
    flat = rn.startswith("b"); core = rn.lstrip("b"); up = core.upper()
    base = ROMAN[up.replace("7", "")] - (1 if flat else 0)
    minor = core == core.lower()
    tri = [0, 3, 7] if minor else [0, 4, 7]
    return [key_midi + base + x for x in tri]


def pluck_layer(n, notes, wave="saw", tau=0.2, cutoff=2000, gain=1.0):
    out = np.zeros(n, np.float32)
    for at, m, dur_, vel in notes:
        a = secs(at); k = min(secs(dur_), n - a)
        if k <= 0: continue
        out[a:a + k] += osc(wave, midi_hz(m), k) * np.exp(-np.arange(k) / (SR * tau)) * vel
    return lp(out, cutoff) * gain


def piano_note(m, k, vel):
    f = midi_hz(m); t = np.arange(k) / SR
    x = sum(np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * h * (1 + 0.0004 * h * h) * t) * a * np.exp(-t / (1.6 / h ** 0.7)) for h, a in [(1, 1), (2, 0.45), (3, 0.25), (4, 0.12), (5, 0.06)])
    x *= np.minimum(1, t / 0.004)
    return (x * vel).astype(np.float32)


def drum(kind, k, seed):
    t = np.arange(k) / SR
    if kind == "kick": f = 45 + 110 * np.exp(-t / 0.035); return (np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * np.exp(-t / 0.22)).astype(np.float32)
    if kind == "snare": return (bp(noise(k, seed), 1200, 6000) * np.exp(-t / 0.09) * 0.7 + np.sin(2 * np.pi * 185 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.06) * 0.5).astype(np.float32)
    if kind == "hat": return (hp(noise(k, seed), 7500) * np.exp(-t / 0.025) * 0.5).astype(np.float32)
    if kind == "clap": return (bp(noise(k, seed), 900, 4000) * (np.exp(-t / 0.012) + 0.6 * np.exp(-np.maximum(0, t - 0.012) / 0.06) * (t > 0.012)) * 0.6).astype(np.float32)
    if kind == "tick": return (bp(noise(k, seed), 2500, 7000) * np.exp(-t / 0.006) * 0.6).astype(np.float32)
    return np.zeros(k, np.float32)


def render_section(mood, intensity, dur, seed=0, key=None):
    M = MOODS[mood]; r = rng(seed); I = float(np.clip(intensity, 0, 1))
    on = {k: I >= v for k, v in M["layers"].items()}
    beat = 60 / M["bpm"]; bar = 4 * beat; clen = bar * M["bars"]
    tail = 3.0; n = secs(dur + tail); key_midi = NOTE[key or M["key"]] + 48
    L = {k: np.zeros(n, np.float32) for k in ["pad", "padR", "sub", "pulse", "arp", "piano", "bell", "drums", "brass", "drone"]}
    starts = np.arange(0, dur, clen)
    plk, arp, pia, bel, brs = [], [], [], [], []
    for ci, cs in enumerate(starts):
        tones = chord_tones(M["prog"][ci % len(M["prog"])], key_midi)
        k = min(secs(clen + 1.6), n - secs(cs)); a = secs(cs)
        if k <= 0: continue
        envp = env_adsr(k, min(1.2, clen * 0.35), 0.5, 0.85, 1.4)
        if on.get("pad"):
            for j, m in enumerate(tones + [tones[0] + 12]):
                for det, side in [(-0.004, "pad"), (0.004, "padR")]:
                    L[side][a:a + k] += osc("saw", midi_hz(m) * (1 + det), k, r.random() * 6.28) * envp * 0.16
        if on.get("sub"): L["sub"][a:a + k] += (sine(midi_hz(tones[0] - 24), k) + 0.25 * sine(midi_hz(tones[0] - 12), k)) * envp * 0.5
        if on.get("drone"): L["drone"][a:a + k] += (osc("saw", midi_hz(key_midi - 12), k) + osc("saw", midi_hz(key_midi - 5), k)) * envp * 0.12
        steps = int(round(clen / (beat / 2)))
        for s in range(steps):
            at = cs + s * beat / 2
            if at >= dur: break
            if on.get("pulse") or on.get("pluck"): plk.append((at, [tones[0], tones[0] + 12, tones[2], tones[0] + 12][s % 4] - (12 if on.get("pulse") else 0), beat * 0.9, 0.55 if s % 2 == 0 else 0.4))
            if on.get("bass") and s % 2 == 0: plk.append((at, tones[0] - 12, beat, 0.7))
            if on.get("arp"):
                for q in range(2):
                    m = (tones + [tones[0] + 12])[(s * 2 + q) % 4] + 12
                    arp.append((at + q * beat / 4, m, beat * 0.6, 0.35 + 0.1 * (q == 0)))
            if on.get("brass") and s == 0: brs.append((at, tones, clen))
        if on.get("piano"):
            for b in range(int(clen / beat)):
                if r.random() < (0.55 if b % 2 == 0 else 0.25):
                    m = int(r.choice(tones)) + 12 + (12 if r.random() < 0.3 else 0)
                    pia.append((cs + b * beat, m, 0.25 + 0.15 * r.random()))
        if on.get("bell") and r.random() < 0.7: bel.append((cs + beat * float(r.choice([0, 1, 2])), tones[int(r.integers(0, 3))] + 24))
        for b in range(int(clen / beat)):
            at = cs + b * beat
            if at >= dur: break
            hits = []
            if on.get("kick") and (b % 2 == 0 or I > 0.85): hits.append(("kick", 0.9))
            if on.get("snare") and b % 2 == 1: hits.append(("snare", 0.6))
            if on.get("clap") and b % 2 == 1: hits.append(("clap", 0.5))
            for kind, v in hits: place(L["drums"], drum(kind, secs(0.4), seed + ci * 31 + b), secs(at), v)
            for h in range(2):
                if on.get("hat"): place(L["drums"], drum("hat", secs(0.1), seed + b + h), secs(at + h * beat / 2), 0.35 if h else 0.25)
                if on.get("tick"): place(L["drums"], drum("tick", secs(0.05), seed + b + h), secs(at + h * beat / 2), 0.35)
    bright = M["bright"] * (0.6 + 0.8 * I)
    pad = np.stack([lp(L["pad"], 500 + 2600 * bright), lp(L["padR"], 500 + 2600 * bright)], 1)
    out = pad + stereo(lp(L["sub"], 200), 0) * 0.9 + stereo(lp(L["drone"], 300 + 800 * bright), 0, 0.6)
    if plk: out += delay(stereo(pluck_layer(n, plk, "saw", 0.16, 900 + 2500 * bright), 0.1), beat * 0.75, 0.3, 0.25)[:n] * 0.55
    if arp: out += delay(stereo(pluck_layer(n, arp, "tri", 0.25, 5000), -0.2), beat * 0.5, 0.35, 0.3)[:n] * 0.35
    for at, m, v in pia:
        a = secs(at); k = min(secs(3.0), n - a)
        if k > 0: out[a:a + k] += stereo(piano_note(m, k, v), (m - 66) / 30) * 0.6
    for at, m in bel:
        a = secs(at); k = min(secs(2.5), n - a)
        if k > 0: out[a:a + k] += stereo(sum(sine(midi_hz(m) * rr_, k) * aa * np.exp(-np.arange(k) / (SR * d)) for rr_, aa, d in [(1, 1, 0.9), (2.76, 0.3, 0.3), (5.4, 0.12, 0.15)]), 0.3) * 0.12
    for at, tones, cl in brs:
        a = secs(at); k = min(secs(cl + 0.8), n - a)
        if k > 0:
            b = sum(osc("saw", midi_hz(m - 12), k) for m in tones[:2]) * env_adsr(k, 0.25, 0.3, 0.8, 0.6) * 0.18
            out[a:a + k] += stereo(lp(b, 1400 * (0.6 + bright)), 0, 0.5)
    out += stereo(lp(L["drums"], 12000), 0) * 0.8
    return reverb(out, 2.8, 0.28, seed).astype(np.float32)


def render_score(plan, total, seed=1, key=None):
    n = secs(total + 0.5); out = np.zeros((n, 2), np.float32); xf = 2.0
    plan = sorted(plan, key=lambda s: s["start"])
    for i, s in enumerate(plan):
        st, en = max(0, s["start"]), min(total, s["end"])
        if en - st < 0.5: continue
        y = render_section(s.get("mood", "mystery"), s.get("intensity", 0.5), en - st + xf, seed + i * 101, key)
        m = len(y); fin = secs(xf if i else 2.5); fo = secs(xf)
        ramp_in = np.sin(np.linspace(0, np.pi / 2, fin)) ** 2
        y[:fin] *= ramp_in[:, None]
        cut = secs(en - st + xf); y = y[:cut]
        if i < len(plan) - 1: y[-fo:] *= (np.cos(np.linspace(0, np.pi / 2, fo)) ** 2)[:, None]
        place(out, y, secs(st - (xf * 0.5 if i else 0)))
    k = secs(3.0); out[-k:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, k)[:, None]
    return (out * db(-20 - rms_db(out))).astype(np.float32)


def default_plan(total):
    return [{"start": 0, "end": total, "mood": "mystery", "intensity": 0.5}]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--project"); ap.add_argument("--mood"); ap.add_argument("--intensity", type=float, default=0.6)
    ap.add_argument("--dur", type=float, default=30); ap.add_argument("--out"); ap.add_argument("--list", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1); ap.add_argument("--key")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.list:
        for k, v in MOODS.items(): print(f"{k:9s} {v['bpm']:>3} bpm  key {v['key']}{'m' if v['minor'] else ''}  {' '.join(v['prog'])}")
        sys.exit()
    if a.project:
        story = json.load(open(os.path.join(a.project, "story", "shots.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
        total = story.get("duration") or story["shots"][-1]["end"]
        plan = story.get("music") or default_plan(total)
        out = a.out or os.path.join(a.project, "build", "music.wav")
        x = render_score(plan, total, a.seed, a.key)
    else:
        x = render_score([{"start": 0, "end": a.dur, "mood": a.mood or "mystery", "intensity": a.intensity}], a.dur, a.seed, a.key)
        out = a.out or f"music_{a.mood}.wav"
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out)), exist_ok=True); write_wav(out, x); print(f"wrote {out}  ({len(x) / SR:.1f}s)")

"""audio/sfx.py — procedural sound-effects library (no downloads, no API keys).

  python audio/sfx.py --list                      names + descriptions
  python audio/sfx.py --demo build/sfx_demo       render every effect to WAV for auditioning
  python audio/sfx.py --make whoosh --out w.wav [--dur 0.8] [--seed 3]

Every effect returns stereo float32 at 48 kHz, peak-normalised to about -1 dBFS.
In story/shots.json a cue looks like {"at": 0.25, "name": "whoosh", "gain": -8, "dur": 0.7, "seed": 2}
("at" is seconds from the shot start; "gain" in dB; dur/seed/pitch are optional).
"""
import argparse, os, sys
import numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
from dsp import (SR, secs, t_axis, noise, lp, hp, bp, sweep_filter, env_adsr, expdec, sine, osc, stereo, as_stereo,
                 reverb, delay, fade, normalize, midi_hz, rng, write_wav)

LIB = {}
def sfx(desc, dur=1.0):
    def deco(fn):
        LIB[fn.__name__] = {"fn": fn, "desc": desc, "dur": dur}; return fn
    return deco


def _thump(n, f0=150, f1=42, tau=0.25, click=0.3, seed=0):
    t = t_axis(n); f = f1 + (f0 - f1) * np.exp(-t / 0.04)
    x = sine(f, n) * expdec(n, tau)
    x[: secs(0.004)] += noise(secs(0.004), seed) * click
    return x


@sfx("air whoosh past camera / quick movement", 0.7)
def whoosh(dur=0.7, seed=1, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = noise(n, seed)
    y = sweep_filter(x, 300 * pitch, 3500 * pitch, q=1.6, curve=0.8) * np.sin(np.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, n)) ** 1.6
    y += sweep_filter(noise(n, seed + 1), 120 * pitch, 900 * pitch, q=0.9) * np.sin(np.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, n)) ** 2 * 0.6
    return normalize(fade(stereo(y, 0, 0.6)), -2)


@sfx("short sharp swish (arm swing, card flick, whip pan)", 0.3)
def swish(dur=0.3, seed=2, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); y = sweep_filter(noise(n, seed), 1200 * pitch, 6000 * pitch, q=2.0, curve=0.6) * np.sin(np.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, n) ** 0.7) ** 2
    return normalize(fade(stereo(y, 0, 0.3)), -3)


@sfx("deep cinematic boom / impact with tail", 2.5)
def impact(dur=2.5, seed=3, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = _thump(n, 120 * pitch, 32 * pitch, 0.6, 0.8, seed) * 1.2
    x += lp(noise(n, seed), 900) * expdec(n, 0.25) * 0.9 + hp(noise(n, seed + 1), 3000) * expdec(n, 0.04) * 0.5
    return normalize(reverb(fade(x, 0, 0.3), 2.6, 0.35, seed), -1)


@sfx("punchy short hit (body hit, ball strike, door slam)", 0.6)
def hit(dur=0.6, seed=4, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = _thump(n, 180 * pitch, 55 * pitch, 0.12, 0.6, seed) + bp(noise(n, seed), 800, 4000) * expdec(n, 0.03) * 0.8
    return normalize(reverb(x, 0.8, 0.15, seed), -1)


@sfx("rising tension sweep into a moment (use 1-3 s before a cut)", 2.0)
def riser(dur=2.0, seed=5, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    y = sweep_filter(noise(n, seed), 300 * pitch, 7000 * pitch, q=2.5, curve=1.5) * u ** 2.2
    y += sine(110 * pitch * 2 ** (u * 2.5), n) * u ** 3 * 0.35 + sine(165 * pitch * 2 ** (u * 2.5), n) * u ** 3 * 0.2
    return normalize(fade(reverb(stereo(y, 0, 0.7), 1.5, 0.25, seed), 0.01, 0.02), -2)


@sfx("downward whoosh + low groan: time slowing down / slow motion begins", 2.2)
def slowmo(dur=2.2, seed=6, **_):
    n = secs(dur); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    y = sweep_filter(noise(n, seed), 4000, 150, q=1.8, curve=0.7) * np.sin(np.pi * u) ** 0.8
    y += sine(220 * 2 ** (-u * 2.2), n) * (1 - u) ** 0.5 * 0.4 * np.minimum(1, u * 10)
    y += sine(55, n) * np.sin(np.pi * u) * 0.5
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(y, 0, 0.6), 3.0, 0.4, seed), -2)


@sfx("time-freeze: reverse swell that snaps into a shimmering high tone", 2.5)
def freeze(dur=2.5, seed=7, **_):
    n = secs(dur); k = int(n * 0.45); u = np.linspace(0, 1, k)
    sw = hp(noise(k, seed), 2500) * u ** 3
    tail_n = n - k; tt = t_axis(tail_n)
    tail = sum(sine(midi_hz(m), tail_n) * a for m, a in [(88, 0.3), (95, 0.2), (100, 0.15), (76, 0.25)]) * np.exp(-tt / 0.9)
    tail += _thump(tail_n, 90, 40, 0.5, 0.2, seed) * 0.8
    return normalize(reverb(np.concatenate([sw, tail]), 3.2, 0.45, seed), -2)


@sfx("clock tick (single)", 0.12)
def tick(dur=0.12, seed=8, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = bp(noise(n, seed), 1800 * pitch, 5000 * pitch) * expdec(n, 0.008) + sine(2400 * pitch, n) * expdec(n, 0.01) * 0.3
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0.1), -6)


@sfx("ticking clock loop (tick-tock every 0.5 s)", 4.0)
def clock(dur=4.0, seed=9, rate=2.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); out = np.zeros((n, 2), np.float32)
    for i, at in enumerate(np.arange(0, dur, 1 / rate)):
        s = tick(pitch=1.0 if i % 2 == 0 else 0.8, seed=seed + i); a = secs(at); m = min(len(s), n - a)
        out[a:a + m] += s[:m]
    return normalize(out, -6)


@sfx("heartbeat lub-dub (loops cleanly at 1 beat/s)", 1.0)
def heartbeat(dur=1.0, seed=10, bpm=60, **_):
    n = secs(dur); out = np.zeros(n, np.float32); beat = 60 / bpm
    for at in np.arange(0, dur, beat):
        for off, g in [(0, 1.0), (0.17, 0.7)]:
            s = _thump(secs(0.25), 70, 38, 0.07, 0.05, seed) * g; a = secs(at + off); m = min(len(s), n - a)
            if m > 0: out[a:a + m] += s[:m]
    return normalize(stereo(lp(out, 300)), -3)


@sfx("UI pop: graphic / label appears", 0.15)
def pop(dur=0.15, seed=11, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); x = sine(500 * pitch * (1 + 1.2 * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.02))), n) * expdec(n, 0.035)
    return normalize(stereo(x), -4)


@sfx("soft UI blip / data beep", 0.12)
def blip(dur=0.12, seed=12, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = (osc("square", 1320 * pitch, n) * 0.4 + sine(1320 * pitch, n)) * env_adsr(n, 0.003, 0.03, 0.4, 0.04)
    return normalize(stereo(lp(x, 6000)), -8)


@sfx("number counter ticking up (rapid blips)", 1.5)
def counter(dur=1.5, seed=13, **_):
    n = secs(dur); out = np.zeros((n, 2), np.float32); at = 0.0; i = 0
    while at < dur - 0.06:
        s = blip(pitch=1 + 0.02 * i, seed=seed); a = secs(at); m = min(len(s), n - a); out[a:a + m] += s[:m] * 0.6; at += 0.07; i += 1
    return normalize(out, -8)


@sfx("bright bell ding / realisation / correct", 2.0)
def ding(dur=2.0, seed=14, pitch=1.0, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); f = 1046 * pitch
    x = sum(sine(f * r, n) * a * np.exp(-t / d) for r, a, d in [(1, 1, 0.9), (2.76, 0.4, 0.4), (5.4, 0.2, 0.2), (8.9, 0.1, 0.1)])
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(x), 2.0, 0.3, seed), -4)


@sfx("magical shimmer / sparkle / superpower reveal", 2.5)
def shimmer(dur=2.5, seed=15, **_):
    n = secs(dur); out = np.zeros(n, np.float32); r = rng(seed)
    for i, m in enumerate([84, 88, 91, 96, 100, 103, 108]):
        a = secs(i * 0.07 + r.random() * 0.03); k = n - a; out[a:] += sine(midi_hz(m), k) * np.exp(-t_axis(k) / 0.6) * 0.3
    out += hp(noise(n, seed), 6000) * np.sin(np.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, n)) * 0.15
    return normalize(reverb(delay(stereo(out), 0.18, 0.4, 0.3)[:n], 2.5, 0.4, seed), -4)


@sfx("dramatic stinger: big low chord hit for reveals / cliffhangers", 3.0)
def stinger(dur=3.0, seed=16, minor=True, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); notes = [38, 45, 50, 53 if minor else 54, 57, 62]
    x = sum(osc("saw", midi_hz(m), n) * 0.18 for m in notes); x = lp(x, 1800) * np.exp(-t / 1.1)
    x += _thump(n, 110, 35, 0.7, 0.6, seed) * 0.9
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(x, 0, 0.5), 3.0, 0.4, seed), -1)


@sfx("footstep (single)", 0.3)
def footstep(dur=0.3, seed=17, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = _thump(n, 120, 60, 0.04, 0.1, seed) * 0.8 + bp(noise(n, seed), 400, 2500) * expdec(n, 0.02) * 0.5
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0.05), -6)


@sfx("ball kick / thud", 0.5)
def kick(dur=0.5, seed=18, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = _thump(n, 220, 70, 0.08, 0.7, seed) + bp(noise(n, seed), 1500, 6000) * expdec(n, 0.012)
    return normalize(reverb(x, 1.0, 0.15, seed), -2)


@sfx("referee whistle (trill)", 1.2)
def whistle(dur=1.2, seed=19, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); f = 2850 + 120 * np.sign(np.sin(2 * np.pi * 28 * t)) * 0.5
    x = (sine(f, n) + hp(noise(n, seed), 2000) * 0.08) * env_adsr(n, 0.02, 0.05, 0.9, 0.12)
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(x), 1.5, 0.2, seed), -6)


@sfx("car passing by (left to right)", 3.0)
def carpass(dur=3.0, seed=20, **_):
    n = secs(dur); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n); prox = np.exp(-((u - 0.5) ** 2) / 0.04)
    eng = osc("saw", 55 * (1.06 - 0.12 * (u > 0.5) * (u - 0.5) * 2), n) * 0.3
    x = (lp(noise(n, seed), 700) * 0.8 + lp(eng, 400)) * prox
    pan = np.clip((u - 0.5) * 2.2, -1, 1); l = np.cos((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4); r = np.sin((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4)
    return normalize(np.stack([x * l, x * r], 1).astype(np.float32), -3)


@sfx("phone vibrating buzz", 1.6)
def phonebuzz(dur=1.6, seed=21, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); gate = ((t % 0.8) < 0.45).astype(np.float32)
    x = lp(osc("square", 160, n), 900) * gate * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 12 * t))
    return normalize(stereo(x), -8)


@sfx("phone ringtone (two-tone)", 2.0)
def phonering(dur=2.0, seed=22, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); gate = ((t % 1.0) < 0.6).astype(np.float32) * ((t % 0.1) < 0.07)
    return normalize(stereo((sine(880, n) + sine(1100, n)) * gate * 0.5), -8)


@sfx("stadium crowd cheer / roar", 4.0)
def cheer(dur=4.0, seed=23, **_):
    n = secs(dur); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n); r = rng(seed)
    x = bp(noise(n, seed, "pink"), 300, 3500) * (0.6 + 0.4 * lp(noise(n, seed + 1), 3))
    for k in range(40):
        f0 = 300 + r.random() * 500; a = secs(r.random() * dur * 0.6); m = min(secs(0.6 + r.random()), n - a)
        if m > 0: x[a:a + m] += sweep_filter(noise(m, seed + k), f0, f0 * 1.6, q=6) * np.sin(np.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, m)) * 0.3
    x *= np.minimum(1, u * 6) * np.minimum(1, (1 - u) * 3)
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(x, 0, 1.0), 2.5, 0.4, seed), -3)


@sfx("applause / slow building clapping crowd", 4.0)
def applause(dur=4.0, seed=24, density=60, **_):
    n = secs(dur); out = np.zeros(n, np.float32); r = rng(seed); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    for _k in range(int(density * dur)):
        a = secs(r.random() * dur); m = min(secs(0.03), n - a)
        if m > 0: out[a:a + m] += bp(noise(m, int(r.integers(1e6))), 800, 5000) * np.exp(-np.arange(m) / (SR * 0.006)) * r.random()
    out *= np.minimum(1, u * 2.5)
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(out, 0, 1.0), 2.0, 0.35, seed), -4)


@sfx("crowd murmur bed (loopable)", 6.0)
def murmur(dur=6.0, seed=25, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = bp(noise(n, seed, "pink"), 200, 1800) * (0.7 + 0.3 * lp(noise(n, seed + 3), 2) * 4)
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0, 1.0), -10)


@sfx("rain ambience bed (loopable)", 6.0)
def rain(dur=6.0, seed=26, heavy=0.5, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = hp(lp(noise(n, seed, "pink"), 7000), 300) * (0.5 + heavy * 0.5); r = rng(seed)
    for _k in range(int(dur * 120 * (0.5 + heavy))):
        a = int(r.integers(0, n - 200)); x[a:a + 120] += hp(noise(120, int(r.integers(1e6))), 2500) * np.exp(-np.arange(120) / 25) * r.random() * 0.6
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0, 1.0), -8)


@sfx("thunder crack + long rumble", 5.0)
def thunder(dur=5.0, seed=27, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n)
    rumble = lp(noise(n, seed, "brown"), 180) * np.exp(-t / 1.8) * (0.6 + 0.4 * lp(noise(n, seed + 1), 4) * 5)
    crack = hp(noise(n, seed + 2), 1500) * np.exp(-t / 0.08) * 0.6
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(rumble * 2 + crack, 0, 0.8), 3.5, 0.4, seed), -1)


@sfx("wind ambience (loopable)", 6.0)
def wind(dur=6.0, seed=28, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = sweep_filter(noise(n, seed), 250, 900, q=3) * (0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.15 * t_axis(n)))
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0, 1.0), -10)


@sfx("quiet room tone / interior air (loopable)", 6.0)
def roomtone(dur=6.0, seed=29, **_):
    n = secs(dur); return normalize(stereo(lp(noise(n, seed, "brown"), 400) + lp(noise(n, seed + 1, "pink"), 2500) * 0.15, 0, 1.0), -18)


@sfx("night city ambience: distant traffic hum (loopable)", 6.0)
def city(dur=6.0, seed=30, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = lp(noise(n, seed, "brown"), 250) * 1.5 + bp(noise(n, seed + 1, "pink"), 400, 1500) * 0.2 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.1 * t_axis(n)))
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0, 1.0), -12)


@sfx("electric hum / lab machine / power-up drone", 3.0)
def hum(dur=3.0, seed=31, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = sum(sine(60 * h, n) * a for h, a in [(1, 0.5), (2, 0.3), (3, 0.2), (5, 0.08)]) * (0.85 + 0.15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 3 * t_axis(n)))
    return normalize(fade(stereo(lp(x, 900)), 0.2, 0.3), -8)


@sfx("power-up: rising electric charge (gadget / machine start)", 2.0)
def powerup(dur=2.0, seed=32, **_):
    n = secs(dur); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n); x = osc("saw", 80 * 2 ** (u * 3), n) * 0.3 + sine(160 * 2 ** (u * 3), n) * 0.3
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(sweep_filter(x, 300, 6500, q=0.8, kind='lp', curve=2.0) * u ** 1.2), 1.5, 0.2, seed), -3)


@sfx("glitch / digital stutter", 0.6)
def glitch(dur=0.6, seed=33, **_):
    n = secs(dur); r = rng(seed); x = np.zeros(n, np.float32); a = 0
    while a < n - 600:
        m = int(r.integers(300, 3000)); m = min(m, n - a); x[a:a + m] = (osc("square", float(r.integers(200, 2000)), m) * 0.5 if r.random() < 0.6 else noise(m, a) * 0.5); a += m + int(r.integers(0, 1500))
    return normalize(stereo(x, 0, 0.5), -6)


@sfx("camera flash / shutter + light pop", 0.5)
def flash(dur=0.5, seed=34, **_):
    n = secs(dur); x = hp(noise(n, seed), 2000) * expdec(n, 0.03) + sine(3000, n) * expdec(n, 0.08) * 0.2
    return normalize(reverb(stereo(x), 1.2, 0.3, seed), -6)


@sfx("insect / fly buzzing past (pans left to right)", 1.5)
def buzz(dur=1.5, seed=35, **_):
    n = secs(dur); t = t_axis(n); u = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    f = 210 + 25 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 7 * t) + 15 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 13 * t)
    x = lp(osc("saw", f, n), 2500) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.abs(np.sin(2 * np.pi * 3 * t))) * np.sin(np.pi * u) ** 0.8
    pan = np.clip(u * 2 - 1, -1, 1); l = np.cos((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4); r = np.sin((pan + 1) * np.pi / 4)
    return normalize(np.stack([x * l, x * r], 1).astype(np.float32), -6)


def make(name, **kw):
    if name not in LIB: raise KeyError(f"unknown sfx '{name}'. Available: {', '.join(LIB)}")
    k = {"dur": LIB[name]["dur"], **{a: b for a, b in kw.items() if b is not None}}
    return as_stereo(LIB[name]["fn"](**k)).astype(np.float32)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--list", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--demo"); ap.add_argument("--make"); ap.add_argument("--out", default="sfx.wav"); ap.add_argument("--dur", type=float); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.list or not (a.demo or a.make):
        for k, v in LIB.items(): print(f"{k:11s} {v['dur']:>4.1f}s  {v['desc']}")
    if a.demo:
        os.makedirs(a.demo, exist_ok=True)
        for k in LIB: write_wav(os.path.join(a.demo, f"{k}.wav"), make(k)); print("wrote", k)
    if a.make:
        write_wav(a.out, make(a.make, dur=a.dur, seed=a.seed)); print("wrote", a.out)

// engine/core.js — shared runtime for every shot. All drawing is in LOGICAL units:
// 16:9 stage = 1920x1080, 9:16 stage = 1080x1920. The player scales to output pixels.
import { STYLES, BASE_MOODS } from './styles.js';

export const E = { W: 1920, H: 1080, PX: 1, fps: 24, vertical: false, style: null, styleId: 'dusk', cfg: {}, words: [], shots: [] };
export const V = (h, v) => (E.vertical ? v : h);           // pick a value by aspect: V(landscape, vertical)

// ---------- math / timing ----------
export const clamp = (x, a = 0, b = 1) => (x < a ? a : x > b ? b : x);
export const lerp = (a, b, k) => a + (b - a) * k;
export const prog = (t, a, b) => clamp((t - a) / (b - a));
export const ease = {
  linear: x => clamp(x),
  in: x => (x = clamp(x)) ** 3,
  out: x => 1 - (1 - clamp(x)) ** 3,
  inOut: x => ((x = clamp(x)) < 0.5 ? 4 * x ** 3 : 1 - (-2 * x + 2) ** 3 / 2),
  sine: x => -(Math.cos(Math.PI * clamp(x)) - 1) / 2,
  outBack: x => { x = clamp(x); const c1 = 1.70158, c3 = c1 + 1; return 1 + c3 * (x - 1) ** 3 + c1 * (x - 1) ** 2; },
  outElastic: x => { x = clamp(x); return x === 0 || x === 1 ? x : 2 ** (-10 * x) * Math.sin((x * 10 - 0.75) * (2 * Math.PI) / 3) + 1; },
};
// keyframes: key(t, [[0, 1], [2, 1.2], [4, 1]], ease.inOut) -> number | array | object of numbers
export function key(t, frames, fn = ease.inOut) {
  if (t <= frames[0][0]) return frames[0][1];
  for (let i = 1; i < frames.length; i++) {
    const [t1, v1] = frames[i];
    if (t <= t1) { const [t0, v0] = frames[i - 1]; return mixVal(v0, v1, (frames[i][2] || fn)((t - t0) / (t1 - t0))); }
  }
  return frames[frames.length - 1][1];
}
export function mixVal(a, b, k) {
  if (typeof a === 'number') return lerp(a, b, k);
  if (Array.isArray(a)) return a.map((v, i) => mixVal(v, b[i], k));
  const o = {}; for (const kk in a) o[kk] = typeof a[kk] === 'number' || Array.isArray(a[kk]) ? mixVal(a[kk], b[kk] ?? a[kk], k) : (k < 0.5 ? a[kk] : b[kk] ?? a[kk]); return o;
}
export function rng(seed = 1) { return () => { seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0; let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed); t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t; return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
const hash = (n) => { const s = Math.sin(n * 127.1 + 311.7) * 43758.5453; return s - Math.floor(s); };
export function noise(x, seed = 0) { const i = Math.floor(x), f = x - i, u = f * f * (3 - 2 * f); return lerp(hash(i + seed * 57), hash(i + 1 + seed * 57), u) * 2 - 1; }
export const shake = (t, amp = 4, freq = 1.3, seed = 3) => [noise(t * freq, seed) * amp, noise(t * freq, seed + 9) * amp];
// talking mouth amount (0..1) driven by the real word timings when available
export function talkAmount(globalT, from = -1, to = 1e9) {
  if (globalT < from || globalT > to) return 0;
  const w = E.words.find(w => globalT >= w.start && globalT <= w.end);
  if (!w && E.words.length) return 0;
  return clamp(0.25 + 0.75 * Math.abs(Math.sin(globalT * 17)) * (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(globalT * 5.3)));
}

// time (global seconds) when a word is spoken: wordTime('brain', 40) -> 43.78 (null if not found)
export function wordTime(match, after = 0) {
  const m = match.toLowerCase().replace(/[^\w']/g, '');
  const w = E.words.find(w => w.start >= after - 0.05 && w.word.toLowerCase().replace(/[^\w']/g, '').startsWith(m));
  return w ? w.start : null;
}

// ---------- colour ----------
export function rgb(c) {
  if (Array.isArray(c)) return c;
  if (c.startsWith('rgb')) { const m = c.match(/[\d.]+/g).map(Number); return [m[0], m[1], m[2], m[3] ?? 1]; }
  let h = c.slice(1); if (h.length === 3) h = [...h].map(x => x + x).join('');
  const n = parseInt(h.slice(0, 6), 16); return [n >> 16, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255, h.length > 6 ? parseInt(h.slice(6), 16) / 255 : 1];
}
export const css = ([r, g, b, a = 1]) => `rgba(${r | 0},${g | 0},${b | 0},${+a.toFixed(3)})`;
export const mix = (c1, c2, k) => { const a = rgb(c1), b = rgb(c2); return css(a.map((v, i) => lerp(v, b[i] ?? 1, k))); };
export const alpha = (c, a) => { const x = rgb(c); return css([x[0], x[1], x[2], a]); };
export const shade = (c, k) => (k < 0 ? mix(c, '#000000', -k) : mix(c, '#ffffff', k));

// ---------- style / moods ----------
export function setStyle(id) { E.styleId = STYLES[id] ? id : 'dusk'; E.style = STYLES[E.styleId]; }
// mood('night') -> { sky:[...], ambient, key, rim, wall, floor, dark, practical, fog }
export function mood(name = 'dusk') { const base = BASE_MOODS[name] || BASE_MOODS.dusk; return { ...base, ...(E.style.moods?.[name] || {}) }; }
export const S = () => E.style;   // current style tokens (ink, feature, ui colours, grade)

// ---------- drawing primitives (logical units) ----------
export function rr(g, x, y, w, h, r) { r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x + r, y); g.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); g.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); g.closePath(); }
export function seg(g, pts, w, col, cap = 'round') { g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = w; g.lineCap = cap; g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]); for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) g.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]); g.stroke(); }
export function poly(g, pts, fill) { g.fillStyle = fill; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]); for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) g.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]); g.closePath(); g.fill(); }
export function circle(g, x, y, r, fill) { g.fillStyle = fill; g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, Math.max(0, r), 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
export function ring(g, x, y, r, w, col) { g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = w; g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, Math.max(0, r), 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); }
export function linGrad(g, x0, y0, x1, y1, stops) { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(x0, y0, x1, y1); stops.forEach((c, i) => Array.isArray(c) ? gr.addColorStop(c[0], c[1]) : gr.addColorStop(i / Math.max(1, stops.length - 1), c)); return gr; }
export function radGrad(g, x, y, r0, r1, stops) { const gr = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, r0, x, y, r1); stops.forEach((c, i) => Array.isArray(c) ? gr.addColorStop(c[0], c[1]) : gr.addColorStop(i / Math.max(1, stops.length - 1), c)); return gr; }
export function glow(g, x, y, r, col, a = 1) { g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.fillStyle = radGrad(g, x, y, 0, r, [alpha(col, a), alpha(col, a * 0.35), alpha(col, 0)]); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.restore(); }
// darken / tint the whole stage (e.g. after a blurred background so the subject pops)
export function veil(g, color = '#000', a = 0.3, op = 'source-over') { g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = op; g.fillStyle = alpha(color, a); g.fillRect(-E.W, -E.H, E.W * 3, E.H * 3); g.restore(); }
export function fillRect(g, x, y, w, h, fill) { g.fillStyle = fill; g.fillRect(x, y, w, h); }
// sky / backdrop gradient over the whole stage (works under any camera)
export function backdrop(g, colors, area = [-E.W, -E.H, E.W * 3, E.H * 3]) { g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, 0, 0, E.H, colors); g.fillRect(...area); }

// ---------- text ----------
export const FONTS = { title: 'Fredoka', ui: 'Nunito', serif: 'Playfair Display' };
export function text(g, str, x, y, o = {}) {
  const { size = 48, font = FONTS.title, weight = 700, color = '#fff', align = 'center', base = 'middle', glow: gl = 0, glowColor = 'rgba(255,140,60,.8)', spacing = 0, alpha: a = 1, stroke = 0, strokeColor = '#000', italic = false } = o;
  g.save(); g.globalAlpha *= a; g.font = `${italic ? 'italic ' : ''}${weight} ${size}px "${font}"`; g.textAlign = align; g.textBaseline = base;
  if ('letterSpacing' in g) g.letterSpacing = `${spacing}px`;
  if (stroke) { g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineWidth = stroke; g.strokeStyle = strokeColor; g.strokeText(str, x, y); }
  if (gl) { g.shadowColor = glowColor; g.shadowBlur = gl; }
  g.fillStyle = color; g.fillText(str, x, y); g.restore();
}
export function measure(g, str, size, font = FONTS.title, weight = 700) { g.save(); g.font = `${weight} ${size}px "${font}"`; const w = g.measureText(str).width; g.restore(); return w; }

// ---------- camera & parallax ----------
// cam = {x, y, z (zoom), r (roll, radians)}; depth 1 = character plane, <1 farther, >1 foreground
export function layer(g, cam, depth, fn) {
  g.save(); const z = 1 + ((cam.z ?? 1) - 1) * depth;
  g.translate(E.W / 2, E.H / 2); if (cam.r) g.rotate(cam.r * Math.min(1, depth)); g.scale(z, z);
  g.translate(-E.W / 2 - (cam.x || 0) * depth, -E.H / 2 - (cam.y || 0) * depth); fn(g); g.restore();
}
// cheap depth-of-field: draw into a small canvas and upscale (optionally extra blur)
const _blurPool = [];
export function blurLayer(g, amount, fn, cam = null, depth = 1) {
  if (amount <= 0.3) { cam ? layer(g, cam, depth, fn) : fn(g); return; }
  const k = clamp(1 / (1 + amount / 3), 0.06, 1);
  const w = Math.max(8, Math.round(E.W * E.PX * k)), h = Math.max(8, Math.round(E.H * E.PX * k));
  let c = _blurPool.find(b => !b.busy && b.width === w && b.height === h);
  if (!c) { c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; _blurPool.push(c); }
  c.busy = true; const x = c.getContext('2d'); x.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); x.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  x.setTransform(w / E.W, 0, 0, h / E.H, 0, 0); cam ? layer(x, cam, depth, fn) : fn(x);
  // blur at the small size (fast), then upscale smoothly
  if (!c.b) { c.b = document.createElement('canvas'); }
  if (c.b.width !== w || c.b.height !== h) { c.b.width = w; c.b.height = h; }
  const bx = c.b.getContext('2d'); bx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); bx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  bx.filter = `blur(${Math.max(0.4, amount * E.PX * k)}px)`; bx.drawImage(c, 0, 0); bx.filter = 'none';
  g.save(); g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
  g.drawImage(c.b, 0, 0, E.W * E.PX, E.H * E.PX); g.restore(); c.busy = false;
}

// ---------- shot registry ----------
const SHOTS = {};
// registerShot('s012', { kind: '2d' | '3d', draw(g, t, info), init?(T, info), overlay?(g, t, info) })
export function registerShot(id, def) { SHOTS[id] = { kind: '2d', ...def }; }
export const getShot = id => SHOTS[id];
export const allShots = () => SHOTS;

// engine/fx2d.js — atmosphere, light and set-dressing helpers (2D canvas, logical units).
import { E, rng, clamp, lerp, alpha, shade, mix, seg, circle, ring, rr, poly, linGrad, radGrad, glow, noise } from './core.js';

// ---------- atmosphere ----------
export function rain(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 1, count = 260, angle = 0.14, speed = 2200, len = 46, color = '#b8c8ff', a = 0.32, w = 1.3, area = [-100, -100, E.W + 200, E.H + 200], timeScale = 1 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed); g.save(); g.strokeStyle = alpha(color, a); g.lineWidth = w; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.beginPath();
  const [ax, ay, aw, ah] = area, dx = Math.sin(angle), dy = Math.cos(angle);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const x0 = ax + r() * aw, y0 = r() * ah, sp = speed * (0.7 + r() * 0.6), L = len * (0.6 + r() * 0.8);
    const y = ay + ((y0 + t * timeScale * sp) % ah), x = x0 + (y - ay) * dx;
    g.moveTo(x, y); g.lineTo(x + dx * L, y + dy * L);
  }
  g.stroke(); g.restore();
}
// frozen raindrops / bubbles hanging in the air (time-stop moments)
export function drops(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 2, count = 70, area = [0, 0, E.W, E.H], rMin = 3, rMax = 14, color = '#cfe0ff', drift = 4 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const x = area[0] + r() * area[2] + Math.sin(t * 0.4 + i) * drift, y = area[1] + r() * area[3] + Math.cos(t * 0.3 + i) * drift, rad = rMin + r() ** 2 * (rMax - rMin);
    g.fillStyle = radGrad(g, x - rad * 0.3, y - rad * 0.3, 0, rad, [alpha(color, 0.05), alpha(color, 0.12), alpha(color, 0.45)]);
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, rad, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); ring(g, x, y, rad, Math.max(0.8, rad * 0.08), alpha(color, 0.55));
    circle(g, x - rad * 0.35, y - rad * 0.35, rad * 0.22, alpha('#ffffff', 0.85));
  }
}
export function dust(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 3, count = 50, color = '#ffe9d2', area = [0, 0, E.W, E.H], size = 2.2, speed = 14, a = 0.5 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed); g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const s = 0.4 + r() * 1.2, ph = r() * 6.28, rad = (0.5 + r()) * size;
    const x = area[0] + ((r() * area[2] + Math.sin(t * 0.5 + ph) * 18) % area[2]), y = area[1] + (((r() * area[3] - t * speed * s) % area[3]) + area[3]) % area[3];
    const al = a * (0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 1.7 + ph)));
    g.fillStyle = radGrad(g, x, y, 0, rad * 3, [alpha(color, al), alpha(color, 0)]); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, rad * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
  }
  g.restore();
}
export function snow(g, t, o = {}) { const { seed = 4, count = 160, color = '#ffffff', speed = 60 } = o; const r = rng(seed); for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { const s = 0.5 + r(), x = (r() * E.W + Math.sin(t * s + i) * 30) % E.W, y = ((r() * E.H + t * speed * s) % (E.H + 20)) - 10; circle(g, x, y, 1.5 + s * 2, alpha(color, 0.75)); } }
export function stars(g, t = 0, o = {}) { const { seed = 5, count = 140, area = [0, 0, E.W, E.H * 0.6], color = '#ffffff' } = o; const r = rng(seed); for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { const x = area[0] + r() * area[2], y = area[1] + r() * area[3], s = r(); circle(g, x, y, 0.6 + s * 1.6, alpha(color, 0.25 + 0.6 * s * (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(t * 2 + i)))); } }
export function fog(g, y, h, color, a = 0.35) { g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, y, 0, y + h, [alpha(color, 0), alpha(color, a), alpha(color, 0)]); g.fillRect(-E.W, y, E.W * 3, h); }

// ---------- light ----------
export function sun(g, x, y, r, color = '#fff6e0', glowColor = '#ffd59a', glowR = 4) { glow(g, x, y, r * glowR, glowColor, 0.55); glow(g, x, y, r * 1.8, '#fff3dc', 0.6); circle(g, x, y, r, color); }
export function moon(g, x, y, r, color = '#e8ecff') { glow(g, x, y, r * 4, color, 0.18); circle(g, x, y, r, color); }
export function godRays(g, x, y, o = {}) {
  const { count = 7, angle = Math.PI / 2, spread = 0.9, len = 1600, color = '#ffd9a0', a = 0.1, t = 0, width = 0.07 } = o;
  g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const ang = angle - spread / 2 + (spread * (i + 0.5)) / count + Math.sin(t * 0.3 + i) * 0.01, w = width * (0.6 + ((i * 37) % 10) / 10);
    g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, x, y, x + Math.cos(ang) * len, y + Math.sin(ang) * len, [alpha(color, a), alpha(color, 0)]);
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y); g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(ang - w) * len, y + Math.sin(ang - w) * len); g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(ang + w) * len, y + Math.sin(ang + w) * len); g.closePath(); g.fill();
  }
  g.restore();
}
// cone of light from a lamp / spotlight / window, pointing at angle
export function lightCone(g, x, y, o = {}) { const { angle = Math.PI / 2, spread = 0.5, len = 700, color = '#ffe2a8', a = 0.18 } = o; g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.fillStyle = radGrad(g, x, y, 0, len, [alpha(color, a), alpha(color, a * 0.4), alpha(color, 0)]); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y); g.arc(x, y, len, angle - spread / 2, angle + spread / 2); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.restore(); }
export function pool(g, x, y, rx, ry, color = '#ffd59a', a = 0.3) { g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.translate(x, y); g.scale(1, ry / rx); g.fillStyle = radGrad(g, 0, 0, 0, rx, [alpha(color, a), alpha(color, 0)]); g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, rx, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.restore(); }
export function lensStreak(g, x, y, w = E.W, color = '#ffe6cc', a = 0.35, h = 2) { g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, x - w / 2, 0, x + w / 2, 0, [alpha(color, 0), alpha(color, a), alpha(color, 0)]); g.fillRect(x - w / 2, y - h / 2, w, h); g.restore(); }
export function sparkle(g, x, y, s = 20, color = '#ffffff', a = 0.9) { g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; glow(g, x, y, s * 0.8, color, a * 0.6); seg(g, [[x - s, y], [x + s, y]], s * 0.06, alpha(color, a)); seg(g, [[x, y - s], [x, y + s]], s * 0.06, alpha(color, a)); g.restore(); }
export function lightning(g, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 9, x1 = E.W * 0.6, y1 = 0, x2 = E.W * 0.5, y2 = E.H * 0.6, color = '#dfe8ff', w = 3, branches = 3, a = 1, jag = 60 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed); const path = (ax, ay, bx, by, n, wd, depth) => {
    const pts = [[ax, ay]]; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) { const u = i / n; pts.push([lerp(ax, bx, u) + (r() - 0.5) * jag * (1 - u * 0.3), lerp(ay, by, u) + (r() - 0.5) * jag * 0.3]); } pts.push([bx, by]);
    g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.shadowColor = color; g.shadowBlur = 24; seg(g, pts, wd, alpha(color, a)); seg(g, pts, wd * 0.4, alpha('#ffffff', a)); g.restore();
    if (depth > 0) for (let b = 0; b < branches; b++) { const k = 2 + Math.floor(r() * (pts.length - 3)); const [px, py] = pts[k]; path(px, py, px + (r() - 0.3) * 260, py + 120 + r() * 200, 6, wd * 0.5, depth - 1); }
  }; path(x1, y1, x2, y2, 14, w, 1);
}
export function speedLines(g, t, o = {}) { const { cx = E.W / 2, cy = E.H / 2, count = 60, color = '#ffffff', a = 0.25, inner = 260, seed = 6 } = o; const r = rng(seed); g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { const ang = r() * Math.PI * 2, d0 = inner + r() * 200 + ((t * 900 + r() * 400) % 500), L = 80 + r() * 260; seg(g, [[cx + Math.cos(ang) * d0, cy + Math.sin(ang) * d0], [cx + Math.cos(ang) * (d0 + L), cy + Math.sin(ang) * (d0 + L)]], 1 + r() * 2.5, alpha(color, a * r())); } g.restore(); }
// expanding rings: someone speaking off-screen / a realisation / a shockwave
export function rings(g, x, y, t, o = {}) { const { count = 3, r0 = 40, r1 = 140, color = '#fff1e6', a = 0.5, speed = 1.4, w = 2 } = o; for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) { const ph = (t * speed + k / count) % 1; ring(g, x, y, r0 + ph * (r1 - r0), w, alpha(color, a * (1 - ph))); } }

export function skyline(g, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 7, x = 0, y = E.H * 0.6, w = E.W, minH = 40, maxH = 220, color = '#2a2440', windowColor = '#ffd59a', lit = 0.35, minW = 26, maxW = 70 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed); let cx = x; g.fillStyle = color;
  while (cx < x + w) { const bw = minW + r() * (maxW - minW), bh = minH + r() ** 1.5 * (maxH - minH); g.fillStyle = color; g.fillRect(cx, y - bh, bw, bh + 2); if (r() < 0.2) g.fillRect(cx + bw * 0.45, y - bh - 24, 3, 24);
    g.fillStyle = windowColor; for (let wy = y - bh + 8; wy < y - 8; wy += 13) for (let wx = cx + 5; wx < cx + bw - 7; wx += 11) if (r() < lit) g.fillRect(wx, wy, 4, 6);
    cx += bw + r() * 6; }
}
export function hills(g, o = {}) { const { seed = 8, y = E.H * 0.7, amp = 80, color = '#2a2440', freq = 0.004 } = o; g.fillStyle = color; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-E.W, E.H * 2); for (let x = -E.W; x <= E.W * 2; x += 20) g.lineTo(x, y - (noise(x * freq, seed) * 0.5 + 0.5) * amp); g.lineTo(E.W * 2, E.H * 2); g.closePath(); g.fill(); }

// window with a view: drawSky(g, x, y, w, h) paints inside, then frame + mullions (+ optional blinds)
export function windowFrame(g, o, drawSky) {
  const { x, y, w, h, cols = 3, rows = 1, frame = '#2b2030', bar = 8, blinds = 0, arch = false } = o;
  g.save(); g.beginPath(); if (arch) { g.moveTo(x, y + h); g.lineTo(x, y + w / 2); g.arc(x + w / 2, y + w / 2, w / 2, Math.PI, 0); g.lineTo(x + w, y + h); g.closePath(); } else g.rect(x, y, w, h);
  g.clip(); drawSky(g, x, y, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < blinds; i++) { g.fillStyle = alpha(frame, 0.35); g.fillRect(x, y + 6 + i * 7, w, 2); }
  g.fillStyle = frame; for (let i = 1; i < cols; i++) g.fillRect(x + (w * i) / cols - bar / 2, y, bar, h); for (let i = 1; i < rows; i++) g.fillRect(x, y + (h * i) / rows - bar / 2, w, bar);

}

export function facade(g, o = {}) { const { x, y, w, h, color = '#2a2238', win = '#ffcf8a', dark = '#1a1424', cols = 4, rows = 3, lit = 0.6, seed = 11, door = false } = o; const r = rng(seed); g.fillStyle = color; g.fillRect(x, y - h, w, h); const cw = w / cols, rh = (h - 30) / rows; for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const on = r() < lit; const wx = x + i * cw + cw * 0.2, wy = y - h + 20 + j * rh + rh * 0.15; g.fillStyle = on ? win : dark; g.fillRect(wx, wy, cw * 0.6, rh * 0.62); if (on) glow(g, wx + cw * 0.3, wy + rh * 0.3, cw * 0.7, win, 0.18); } if (door) { g.fillStyle = win; g.fillRect(x + w / 2 - 26, y - 90, 52, 90); glow(g, x + w / 2, y - 45, 120, win, 0.3); } }

// stadium / audience crowd: rows of tiny bobbing stick people
export function crowd(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { seed = 12, x = 0, y = E.H * 0.5, w = E.W, rows = 6, rowH = 26, size = 1, color = '#3a3358', colors = null, flags = 0.02, wave = 0, cheer = 0 } = o;
  const r = rng(seed);
  for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const ry = y + j * rowH, sc = size * (1 + j * 0.06); for (let cx = x + r() * 10; cx < x + w; cx += 13 * sc + r() * 6) {
    const c = colors ? colors[Math.floor(r() * colors.length)] : shade(color, (r() - 0.5) * 0.3); const ph = r() * 6.28, bob = Math.max(0, Math.sin(t * 6 + ph)) * 3 * cheer + (wave ? Math.max(0, Math.sin(t * 3 - cx * 0.01 * wave)) * 8 : 0);
    circle(g, cx, ry - 14 * sc - bob, 5 * sc, c); seg(g, [[cx, ry - 9 * sc - bob], [cx, ry + 6 * sc]], 5 * sc, c);
    if (cheer > 0.5 && r() < 0.3) seg(g, [[cx, ry - 6 * sc - bob], [cx - 5 * sc, ry - 22 * sc - bob]], 2 * sc, c);
    if (r() < flags) { const fc = ['#e04a3a', '#3a7ae0', '#f2c21a', '#ffffff'][Math.floor(r() * 4)]; seg(g, [[cx + 4, ry - 10 - bob], [cx + 4, ry - 40 - bob]], 1.5, '#ddd'); g.fillStyle = fc; g.fillRect(cx + 4, ry - 40 - bob + Math.sin(t * 4 + ph) * 2, 18, 11); }
  } }
}

async function loadFonts() {
  const F = [['Fredoka', '600', 'fredoka/files/fredoka-latin-600-normal.woff2'], ['Fredoka', '700', 'fredoka/files/fredoka-latin-700-normal.woff2'],
    ['Nunito', '800', 'nunito/files/nunito-latin-800-normal.woff2'], ['Nunito', '900', 'nunito/files/nunito-latin-900-normal.woff2'],
    ['Playfair Display', '900', 'playfair-display/files/playfair-display-latin-900-italic.woff2', 'italic']];
  for (const [fam, w, file, style] of F) { try { const ff = new FontFace(fam, `url(/node_modules/@fontsource/${file})`, { weight: w, style: style || 'normal' }); await ff.load(); document.fonts.add(ff); } catch (e) { console.log('font missing', fam, w); } }
}
async function boot() {

  const vertical = (cfg.aspect || '16:9') === '9:16';
  const short = RES[cfg.resolution || '1080p'] || 1080, long = Math.round((short * 16) / 9 / 2) * 2;
  const scale = +(q.get('scale') || 1);
  const outW = Math.round(((vertical ? short : long) * scale) / 2) * 2, outH = Math.round(((vertical ? long : short) * scale) / 2) * 2;
  Object.assign(E, { vertical, W: vertical ? 1080 : 1920, H: vertical ? 1920 : 1080, fps: cfg.fps || 24, cfg, words: words.words || words, shots: story.shots, outW, outH });
  E.PX = outW / E.W;
  setStyle(q.get('style') || cfg.style || 'dusk');
  await loadFonts();
  main = document.getElementById('main'); main.width = outW; main.height = outH; mg = main.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  buildLUT();
  bufA = mk(outW, outH); bufB = mk(outW, outH);
  const gw = Math.ceil(outW / 2), gh = Math.ceil(outH / 2);
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const c = mk(gw, gh), x = c.getContext('2d'), id = x.createImageData(gw, gh); let s = 1234 + k * 999; for (let i = 0; i < id.data.length; i += 4) { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; const v = s >>> 24; id.data[i] = id.data[i + 1] = id.data[i + 2] = v; id.data[i + 3] = 255; } x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); grainTiles.push(c); }
  const list = await (await fetch(`/__manifest`)).json();
  for (const f of list) { try { await import(f); } catch (e) { errors.push(`import ${f}: ${e.message}`); console.log('IMPORT ERROR', f, e.message); } }
  if (E.shots.some(s => getShot(s.id)?.kind === '3d')) { three = await import('./three-kit.js'); glCanvas = mk(outW, outH); three.getRenderer(glCanvas); }
  const dur = story.duration || E.shots[E.shots.length - 1].end;
  window.DURATION = dur; window.TOTAL_FRAMES = Math.ceil(dur * E.fps); window.OUT = [outW, outH];
  window.SHOT_LIST = E.shots.map(s => ({ id: s.id, start: s.start, end: s.end, ok: !!getShot(s.id) }));
  window.READY = true;
}

// colour grade: per-channel lift/gamma/gain + contrast as LUTs, saturation per pixel
let LUT = null, SAT = 1;
function buildLUT() {
  const gr = E.style.grade || {}; const lift = gr.lift || [0, 0, 0], gam = gr.gamma || [1, 1, 1], gain = gr.gain || [1, 1, 1], con = gr.contrast ?? 1; SAT = gr.sat ?? 1;
  const ident = con === 1 && lift.every(v => v === 0) && gam.every(v => v === 1) && gain.every(v => v === 1);
  if (ident && SAT === 1) { LUT = null; return; }
  LUT = [0, 1, 2].map(c => { const a = new Uint8ClampedArray(256); for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { let x = i / 255; x = clamp((x - 0.5) * con + 0.5); x = clamp(gain[c] * (x + lift[c] * (1 - x))); x = Math.pow(x, 1 / gam[c]); a[i] = Math.round(x * 255); } return a; });
}
function applyGrade(W, H) {
  if (!LUT) return; const img = mg.getImageData(0, 0, W, H), d = img.data, [R, G, B] = LUT, s = SAT;
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) {
    let r = R[d[i]], g = G[d[i + 1]], b = B[d[i + 2]];
    if (s !== 1) { const l = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b; r = l + (r - l) * s; g = l + (g - l) * s; b = l + (b - l) * s; }
    d[i] = r; d[i + 1] = g; d[i + 2] = b;
  }
  mg.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
}
function shotIndexAt(t) { const S = E.shots; for (let i = 0; i < S.length; i++) if (t < S[i].end) return i; return S.length - 1; }
const cache3D = new Map();
function state3D(shot, def, info) {
  if (cache3D.has(shot.id)) return cache3D.get(shot.id);
  for (const [k, v] of cache3D) if (cache3D.size >= 2) { v.dispose?.(); v.stage?.dispose?.(); cache3D.delete(k); }
  const st = def.init(three, info); cache3D.set(shot.id, st); return st;
}
function drawShot(buf, shot, lt) {
  const g = buf.getContext('2d'); g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); g.globalAlpha = 1; g.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over'; g.filter = 'none';
  g.fillStyle = '#000'; g.fillRect(0, 0, buf.width, buf.height); g.setTransform(E.PX, 0, 0, E.PX, 0, 0);
  const def = getShot(shot.id); const dur = shot.end - shot.start;
  const info = { ...(shot.params || {}), shot, dur, globalT: shot.start + lt, p: clamp(lt / dur), line: shot.line };
  if (!def) { g.fillStyle = '#20141c'; g.fillRect(0, 0, E.W, E.H); text(g, `MISSING SHOT ${shot.id}`, E.W / 2, E.H / 2 - 30, { size: 60, color: '#ff6f61' }); text(g, (shot.line || '').slice(0, 80), E.W / 2, E.H / 2 + 50, { size: 30, color: '#fff', font: FONTS.ui, weight: 800 }); return; }
  try {
    if (def.kind === '3d') {
      const st = state3D(shot, def, info); def.draw(st, lt, info, three); (st.stage || st).render();
      g.save(); g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); g.drawImage(glCanvas, 0, 0, buf.width, buf.height); g.restore();
      if (def.overlay) { g.save(); def.overlay(g, lt, info, st); g.restore(); }
    } else { g.save(); def.draw(g, lt, info); g.restore(); }
  } catch (e) { const msg = `${shot.id}@${lt.toFixed(2)}: ${e.message}`; if (!errors.includes(msg) && errors.length < 200) errors.push(msg); console.log('SHOT ERROR', msg, e.stack?.split('\n')[1]);
    g.setTransform(E.PX, 0, 0, E.PX, 0, 0); text(g, 'ERROR ' + msg.slice(0, 90), E.W / 2, E.H / 2, { size: 34, color: '#ff4040', font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900 }); }
}
const BARS = () => ({ none: 0, wide: V(60, 0), scope: V(138, 170), thin: V(30, 60) });
const barVal = (s) => (typeof (s?.bars ?? E.cfg.bars) === 'number' ? (s.bars ?? E.cfg.bars) : BARS()[s?.bars ?? E.cfg.bars ?? V('wide', 'none')] ?? 0);

window.renderFrame = function (f) {
  const t = f / E.fps, i = shotIndexAt(t), shot = E.shots[i], lt = t - shot.start;
  drawShot(bufA, shot, lt);
  const tr = shot.in || { type: 'cut' }, tdur = tr.dur ?? (tr.type === 'whip' ? 0.35 : 0.5);
  const W = main.width, H = main.height; mg.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); mg.globalAlpha = 1; mg.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  const grade = E.style.grade; mg.filter = grade.filter && grade.filter !== 'none' ? grade.filter : 'none';
  if (tr.type !== 'cut' && i > 0 && lt < tdur) {
    const prev = E.shots[i - 1], k = ease.inOut(lt / tdur); drawShot(bufB, prev, t - prev.start);
    if (tr.type === 'fade') { mg.drawImage(bufB, 0, 0); mg.globalAlpha = k; mg.drawImage(bufA, 0, 0); }
    else if (tr.type === 'dip' || tr.type === 'dipWhite') { const c = tr.type === 'dip' ? '#000' : '#fff'; mg.drawImage(k < 0.5 ? bufB : bufA, 0, 0); mg.filter = 'none'; mg.globalAlpha = k < 0.5 ? k * 2 : (1 - k) * 2; mg.fillStyle = c; mg.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
    else if (tr.type === 'flash') { mg.drawImage(bufA, 0, 0); mg.filter = 'none'; mg.globalAlpha = (1 - lt / tdur) ** 2; mg.fillStyle = '#fff'; mg.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
    else if (tr.type === 'whip') { const dir = tr.dir || 1; for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) { mg.globalAlpha = 0.3; mg.drawImage(bufB, -dir * k * W * 1.1 - dir * s * W * 0.02, 0); mg.drawImage(bufA, dir * (1 - k) * W * 1.1 + dir * s * W * 0.02, 0); } }
    else if (tr.type === 'zoom') { mg.drawImage(bufB, 0, 0); const s = 1.15 - 0.15 * k; mg.globalAlpha = k; mg.translate(W / 2, H / 2); mg.scale(s, s); mg.drawImage(bufA, -W / 2, -H / 2); mg.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); }
    else mg.drawImage(bufA, 0, 0);
  } else mg.drawImage(bufA, 0, 0);
  mg.globalAlpha = 1; mg.filter = 'none'; mg.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  applyGrade(W, H);
  if (grade.tint) { mg.globalCompositeOperation = grade.tint[1]; mg.fillStyle = alpha(grade.tint[0], grade.tint[2]); mg.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); mg.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over'; }
  if (shot.vignette !== false && grade.vignette > 0) { const v = mg.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.35, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.62); v.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); v.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${grade.vignette})`); mg.fillStyle = v; mg.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (grade.grain > 0) { mg.save(); mg.globalAlpha = grade.grain; mg.globalCompositeOperation = 'overlay'; mg.drawImage(grainTiles[f % 4], 0, 0, W, H); mg.restore(); }
  mg.setTransform(E.PX, 0, 0, E.PX, 0, 0);
  if (E.cfg.captions && shot.captions !== false) captions(mg, t, E.cfg.captionStyle || {});
  const cur = barVal(shot), prevB = i > 0 ? barVal(E.shots[i - 1]) : cur, bar = prevB + (cur - prevB) * ease.inOut(prog(lt, 0, 0.6));
  if (bar > 0) { mg.fillStyle = '#000'; mg.fillRect(0, 0, E.W, bar); mg.fillRect(0, E.H - bar, E.W, bar); }
  const fi = E.cfg.fadeIn ?? 0.5, fo = E.cfg.fadeOut ?? 0.8, dur = window.DURATION, fade = Math.max(fi > 0 ? 1 - t / fi : 0, fo > 0 ? 1 - (dur - t) / fo : 0);
  if (fade > 0) { mg.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp(fade)})`; mg.fillRect(0, 0, E.W, E.H); }
  if (q.has('debug')) { mg.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.6)'; mg.fillRect(0, E.H - 54, E.W, 54); text(mg, `${shot.id}  ${t.toFixed(2)}s  (${lt.toFixed(2)}/${(shot.end - shot.start).toFixed(2)})  ${(shot.line || '').slice(0, 70)}`, 20, E.H - 27, { size: 26, color: '#fff', font: FONTS.ui, weight: 800, align: 'left' }); }
  mg.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
};
boot().catch(e => { window.BOOT_ERROR = e.message + '\n' + e.stack; console.log('BOOT ERROR', e.message); });
