// ---------- pose helpers ----------
export const pose = (name) => POSES[name] || POSES.stand;
export const blendPose = (a, b, k) => mixVal(typeof a === 'string' ? pose(a) : a, typeof b === 'string' ? pose(b) : b, ease.inOut(k));
// posesAt(t, [[0,'stand'],[1.2,'talk'],[3,'point']], 0.35) -> holds each pose, blends over `blend` s
export function posesAt(t, keys, blend = 0.35) {
  let cur = keys[0][1];
  for (let i = 1; i < keys.length; i++) {
    const [ti, pi] = keys[i];
    if (t < ti) break;
    const k = clamp((t - ti) / blend); cur = k >= 1 ? pi : blendPose(cur, pi, k);
    if (k < 1) return cur;
  }
  return typeof cur === 'string' ? pose(cur) : cur;
}
// small idle life: breathing + sway, applied on top of any pose
export function breathe(p, t, amt = 1, seed = 0) {
  const b = Math.sin(t * 2.1 + seed) * 1.6 * amt, s = Math.sin(t * 0.9 + seed * 2) * 1.2 * amt;
  const q = { ...p }; for (const k of ['head', 'sh', 'eF', 'hF', 'eB', 'hB']) q[k] = [p[k][0] + s * (k === 'head' ? 1.2 : 0.6), p[k][1] + b]; return q;
}
function ik(a, b, L1, L2, dir = 1) {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1]; let d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); d = Math.min(d, L1 + L2 - 0.01);
  const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - dir * Math.acos(clamp((L1 * L1 + d * d - L2 * L2) / (2 * L1 * d), -1, 1));
  return [a[0] + Math.cos(ang) * L1, a[1] + Math.sin(ang) * L1];
}
// walk / run cycles. phase advances ~ 2π per step pair: phase = t * speed * 2π
export function walkPose(phase, o = {}) {
  const { stride = 26, lift = 12, bob = 3, arm = 16, lean = 2, run = false } = o;
  const st = run ? 46 : stride, li = run ? 34 : lift, bb = run ? 7 : bob, ar = run ? 30 : arm, le = run ? 14 : lean;
  const hipY = -100 + (run ? 6 : 0) - bb * (1 - Math.abs(Math.sin(phase))) + bb * 0.5;
  const hip = [0, hipY], sh = [le, hipY - 74], head = [le + 4, hipY - 108];
  const foot = (ph) => [st * Math.sin(ph), -li * Math.max(0, Math.cos(ph))];
  const fF = foot(phase), fB = foot(phase + Math.PI);
  const kF = ik(hip, fF, 52, 52, 1), kB = ik(hip, fB, 52, 52, 1);
  const hand = (ph) => run ? [le + ar * Math.sin(ph) * 0.9 + 6, hipY - 46 - Math.cos(ph) * 10] : [le * 0.5 - ar * Math.sin(ph) + 4, hipY - 2];
  const hF = hand(phase + Math.PI), hB = hand(phase);
  const elbow = (h) => run ? [lerp(sh[0], h[0], 0.5) - 14, lerp(sh[1], h[1], 0.5) + 6] : [lerp(sh[0], h[0], 0.5) - 3, sh[1] + 38];
  return { head, sh, hip, eF: elbow(hF), hF, eB: elbow(hB), hB, kF, fF, kB, fB };
}

// ---------- faces ----------
export const EXPR = {
  neutral: { eyes: 'dot', brows: null, mouth: 'smallSmile' }, happy: { eyes: 'happy', brows: null, mouth: 'grin' }, joy: { eyes: 'happy', brows: 'raised', mouth: 'grin' },
  smile: { eyes: 'dot', brows: null, mouth: 'smile' }, surprised: { eyes: 'ring', brows: 'raised', mouth: 'o' }, shocked: { eyes: 'wide', brows: 'raised', mouth: 'oBig' },
  scared: { eyes: 'ring', brows: 'worried', mouth: 'wavy' }, worried: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'worried', mouth: 'wavy' }, nervous: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'worried', mouth: 'flat', sweat: true },
  angry: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'angry', mouth: 'flat' }, determined: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'angry', mouth: 'smallFlat' }, sad: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'worried', mouth: 'frown' },
  cry: { eyes: 'closed', brows: 'worried', mouth: 'frown', tears: true }, smirk: { eyes: 'dot', brows: null, mouth: 'smirk' }, sleepy: { eyes: 'closed', brows: null, mouth: 'flat' },
  thinking: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'raised', mouth: 'smallFlat', look: [0.5, -0.6] }, confused: { eyes: 'dot', brows: 'confused', mouth: 'wavy' }, awe: { eyes: 'wide', brows: 'raised', mouth: 'o' },
};
// face(g, headX, headY, r, {expr, talk, blink, look:[x,y], front, color, facing})  — also used for giant close-ups
export function face(g, hx, hy, r, o = {}) {
  const ex = { ...(EXPR[o.expr] || EXPR.neutral), ...(o.override || {}) };
  const col = o.color || S().feature, ink = S().ink;
  const fx = hx + (o.front ? 0 : r * 0.34), fy = hy - r * 0.08;
  const look = o.look || ex.look || [0, 0];
  const eyeX = o.front ? [fx - r * 0.34, fx + r * 0.34] : [fx - r * 0.26, fx + r * 0.32];
  const ey = fy - r * 0.08;
  g.save(); g.lineCap = 'round'; g.lineJoin = 'round';
  eyeX.forEach((x, i) => {
    const s = o.front ? 1 : i === 0 ? 0.88 : 1;
    if (o.blink || ex.eyes === 'closed') { seg(g, [[x - r * 0.12 * s, ey], [x + r * 0.12 * s, ey]], r * 0.08, col); return; }
    switch (ex.eyes) {
      case 'happy': g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.09; g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, ey + r * 0.05, r * 0.13 * s, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.9); g.stroke(); break;
      case 'ring': ring(g, x, ey, r * 0.2 * s, r * 0.085, col); circle(g, x + look[0] * r * 0.06, ey + look[1] * r * 0.06, r * 0.075 * s, col); break;
      case 'wide': circle(g, x, ey, r * 0.2 * s, col); circle(g, x + look[0] * r * 0.08, ey + look[1] * r * 0.08, r * 0.085 * s, ink); break;
      default: circle(g, x + look[0] * r * 0.05, ey + look[1] * r * 0.05, r * 0.125 * s, col);
    }
  });
  const by = ey - r * 0.3, bw = r * 0.15;
  const brow = (x, a) => seg(g, [[x - bw, by + a], [x + bw, by - a]], r * 0.075, col);
  if (ex.brows === 'worried') { brow(eyeX[0], -r * 0.06); brow(eyeX[1], r * 0.06); }
  if (ex.brows === 'angry') { brow(eyeX[0], r * 0.08); brow(eyeX[1], -r * 0.08); }
  if (ex.brows === 'raised') eyeX.forEach(x => { g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.07; g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, by + r * 0.12, r * 0.15, Math.PI * 1.2, Math.PI * 1.8); g.stroke(); });
  if (ex.brows === 'confused') { brow(eyeX[0], r * 0.07); g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.07; g.beginPath(); g.arc(eyeX[1], by + r * 0.08, r * 0.15, Math.PI * 1.2, Math.PI * 1.8); g.stroke(); }
  const mx = fx + (o.front ? 0 : r * 0.04), my = fy + r * 0.42;
  const talk = o.talk || 0;
  if (talk > 0.05) { g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(mx, my, r * 0.16, r * (0.05 + 0.2 * talk), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
  else switch (ex.mouth) {
    case 'grin': g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(mx - r * 0.3, my - r * 0.06); g.quadraticCurveTo(mx, my + r * 0.42, mx + r * 0.3, my - r * 0.06); g.closePath(); g.fill(); break;
    case 'smile': g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.09; g.beginPath(); g.arc(mx, my - r * 0.2, r * 0.27, 0.35, Math.PI - 0.35); g.stroke(); break;
    case 'smallSmile': g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.085; g.beginPath(); g.arc(mx, my - r * 0.14, r * 0.18, 0.4, Math.PI - 0.4); g.stroke(); break;
    case 'o': g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(mx, my, r * 0.11, r * 0.15, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); break;
    case 'oBig': ring(g, mx, my + r * 0.04, r * 0.15, r * 0.08, col); break;
    case 'flat': seg(g, [[mx - r * 0.2, my], [mx + r * 0.2, my]], r * 0.085, col); break;
    case 'smallFlat': seg(g, [[mx - r * 0.12, my], [mx + r * 0.12, my]], r * 0.085, col); break;
    case 'frown': g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.085; g.beginPath(); g.arc(mx, my + r * 0.2, r * 0.2, Math.PI + 0.5, Math.PI * 2 - 0.5); g.stroke(); break;
    case 'smirk': g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.085; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(mx - r * 0.16, my); g.quadraticCurveTo(mx + r * 0.05, my + r * 0.06, mx + r * 0.22, my - r * 0.1); g.stroke(); break;
    case 'wavy': { g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = r * 0.085; g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i <= 16; i++) { const u = i / 16; const x = mx - r * 0.24 + u * r * 0.48, y = my + Math.sin(u * Math.PI * 2) * r * 0.06; i ? g.lineTo(x, y) : g.moveTo(x, y); } g.stroke(); break; }
  }
  if (ex.tears) { g.fillStyle = '#8fd0ff'; for (const x of eyeX) { g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x, ey + r * 0.3, r * 0.05, r * 0.1, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); } }
  if (ex.sweat) { g.fillStyle = '#9fd8ff'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx - r * 0.6, hy - r * 0.45, r * 0.08, r * 0.13, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
  g.restore();
}

// ---------- outfits ----------
// outfit = { top: 'hoodie'|'labcoat'|'trench'|'jersey'|'tshirt'|'suit'|'dress'|'tracksuit'|'none', color, color2,
//            legs: colour (trousers) | null, head: 'cap'|'capBack'|'beanie'|'fedora'|'headband'|'visor'|'hood'|'ponytail'|'bun'|'curly'|'spiky'|'none',
//            headColor, face: 'glasses'|'sunglasses'|null, gloves: colour|null, number: '1', kid: bool }
function drawHeadwear(g, o, hx, hy, r, pass) {
  const c = o.headColor || '#f2c21a', dark = shade(c, -0.25), hi = shade(c, 0.35);
  const sil = pass === 'sil';
  const fill = (col) => (sil ? o._sil : col);
  switch (o.head) {
    case 'cap': case 'capBack': {
      const back = o.head === 'capBack' ? -1 : 1;
      g.fillStyle = fill(c); g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx - 1, hy - r * 0.32, r * 1.04, r * 0.82, 0, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
      seg(g, [[hx + back * r * 0.25, hy - r * 0.34], [hx + back * r * 1.55, hy - r * 0.26]], r * 0.26, fill(dark));
      if (!sil) { g.strokeStyle = alpha(hi, 0.8); g.lineWidth = r * 0.08; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx - r * 0.1, hy - r * 0.5, r * 0.6, r * 0.42, 0, Math.PI * 1.15, Math.PI * 1.55); g.stroke(); }
      break; }
    case 'visor': seg(g, [[hx - r * 0.95, hy - r * 0.42], [hx + r * 0.9, hy - r * 0.42]], r * 0.22, fill(c)); seg(g, [[hx + r * 0.3, hy - r * 0.4], [hx + r * 1.5, hy - r * 0.3]], r * 0.22, fill(dark)); break;
    case 'beanie': {
      g.fillStyle = fill(c); g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx - 1, hy - r * 0.18, r * 1.06, r * 1.0, 0, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
      rr(g, hx - r * 1.08, hy - r * 0.34, r * 2.16, r * 0.34, r * 0.14); g.fillStyle = fill(dark); g.fill();
      circle(g, hx - r * 0.1, hy - r * 1.2, r * 0.3, fill(hi)); break; }
    case 'fedora': {
      g.fillStyle = fill(c); g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx, hy - r * 0.55, r * 1.8, r * 0.3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
      rr(g, hx - r * 0.78, hy - r * 1.45, r * 1.56, r * 0.95, r * 0.25); g.fill();
      if (!sil) { g.fillStyle = dark; g.fillRect(hx - r * 0.78, hy - r * 0.78, r * 1.56, r * 0.2); g.strokeStyle = alpha(hi, 0.6); g.lineWidth = r * 0.06; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(hx, hy - r * 0.55, r * 1.8, r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 1.05, Math.PI * 1.6); g.stroke(); }
      break; }
    case 'headband': {
      g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(hx, hy, r * 1.04, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.clip(); g.fillStyle = fill(c); g.fillRect(hx - r * 1.2, hy - r * 0.62, r * 2.4, r * 0.3); g.restore();
      seg(g, [[hx - r * 0.95, hy - r * 0.48], [hx - r * 1.5, hy - r * 0.2], [hx - r * 1.7, hy + r * 0.1]], r * 0.16, fill(c)); break; }
    case 'ponytail': { g.strokeStyle = fill(o._ink); g.lineWidth = r * 0.42; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(hx - r * 0.7, hy - r * 0.55); g.quadraticCurveTo(hx - r * 1.7, hy - r * 0.4, hx - r * 1.45, hy + r * 0.75); g.stroke();
      if (o.headColor && !sil) seg(g, [[hx - r * 0.9, hy - r * 0.75], [hx - r * 0.8, hy - r * 0.35]], r * 0.22, o.headColor); break; }
    case 'bun': circle(g, hx - r * 0.55, hy - r * 0.95, r * 0.45, fill(o._ink)); break;
    case 'curly': for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const a = Math.PI * (1.05 + i * 0.13); circle(g, hx + Math.cos(a) * r * 0.92, hy + Math.sin(a) * r * 0.92, r * 0.34, fill(o._ink)); } break;
    case 'spiky': g.fillStyle = fill(o._ink); g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) { const a = Math.PI * (1.05 + i * 0.15); const rr2 = i % 2 ? r * 1.45 : r * 0.95; g.lineTo(hx + Math.cos(a) * rr2, hy + Math.sin(a) * rr2); } g.closePath(); g.fill(); break;
  }
}
function torsoWear(g, o, p, pass) {
  const sil = pass === 'sil', c = sil ? o._sil : o.color || '#d64a33', d = sil ? o._sil : shade(o.color || '#d64a33', -0.22);
  const [sx, sy] = p.sh, [hx, hy] = p.hip;
  switch (o.top) {
    case 'hoodie': case 'tracksuit': case 'jersey': case 'tshirt': case 'suit': case 'dress': {
      const col = o.top === 'suit' ? (sil ? o._sil : o.color || '#2a2833') : c;
      const top = sy - 7, bot = hy + (o.top === 'jersey' || o.top === 'tshirt' ? 4 : 12);
      g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(sx - 15, top + 6); g.quadraticCurveTo(sx, top - 4, sx + 15, top + 6); g.lineTo(hx + 15, bot); g.lineTo(hx - 15, bot); g.closePath(); g.fill();
      if (o.top === 'dress') poly(g, [[hx - 14, hy - 8], [hx + 14, hy - 8], [hx + 34, hy + 50], [hx - 34, hy + 50]], col);
      if (!sil && o.top === 'hoodie') { g.fillStyle = d; g.fillRect(hx - 15, bot - 7, 30, 7); seg(g, [[sx + 6, sy - 2], [sx + 7, sy + 18]], 2, shade(c, 0.4)); }
      if (!sil && o.top === 'suit') { poly(g, [[sx - 1, sy - 4], [sx + 4, sy - 4], [sx + 5, sy + 40], [sx + 1, sy + 46]], o.color2 || '#c0392b'); }
      if (!sil && o.top === 'jersey' && o.number) { g.fillStyle = o.color2 || '#fff'; g.font = '700 24px Nunito'; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.fillText(o.number, (sx + hx) / 2 + 2, (sy + hy) / 2); }
      if (!sil && o.top === 'tracksuit') seg(g, [[sx + 12, sy + 2], [hx + 12, hy]], 3, o.color2 || '#ffffff');
      break; }
    case 'labcoat': case 'trench': {
      const col = sil ? o._sil : o.top === 'labcoat' ? '#ebe7f0' : o.color || '#2c2833';
      g.fillStyle = col; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(sx - 15, sy - 5); g.lineTo(sx + 15, sy - 5); g.lineTo(hx + 25, hy + 44); g.lineTo(hx - 23, hy + 44); g.closePath(); g.fill();
      if (!sil) { seg(g, [[sx + 3, sy], [hx + 4, hy + 42]], 2, o.top === 'labcoat' ? '#a9a2b5' : shade(col, 0.25)); seg(g, [[sx - 6, sy - 3], [sx + 2, sy + 22], [sx + 10, sy - 3]], 2.2, o.top === 'labcoat' ? '#c9c2d4' : shade(col, 0.3));
        if (o.top === 'trench') g.fillStyle = shade(col, -0.3), g.fillRect(hx - 18, hy - 6, 38, 6); }
      break; }
  }
}
function sleeve(o) { if (['hoodie', 'tracksuit', 'labcoat', 'trench', 'suit'].includes(o.top)) return 'long'; if (['jersey', 'tshirt'].includes(o.top)) return 'short'; return null; }
function sleeveColor(o, far) { const c = o.top === 'labcoat' ? '#f3f0f6' : o.top === 'trench' ? o.color || '#2c2833' : o.top === 'suit' ? o.color || '#2a2833' : o.color || '#d64a33'; return far ? shade(c, -0.3) : c; }

// ---------- main draw ----------
// drawFigure(g, { x, y, s, facing, pose, rot, outfit, expr, talk, blink, look, rim:{dir:[x,y], color, w}, dim, alpha, prop, shadow })
export function drawFigure(g, o) {
  const st = S(), ink = o.ink || st.ink, far = o.inkFar || st.inkFar;
  const p = o.pose || POSES.stand, s = (o.s ?? 1) * (o.outfit?.kid ? 0.72 : 1), f = o.facing ?? 1;
  const of = { ...(o.outfit || {}), _ink: ink };
  const rim = o.rim === false ? null : { dir: [-0.8, -0.5], w: st.rimWidth, color: '#ffc68a', ...(o.rim || {}) };
  g.save(); if (o.alpha != null) g.globalAlpha *= o.alpha;
  if (o.shadow !== false && !o.rot) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.28)'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(o.x, o.y + 2, 34 * s, 6 * s, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
  if (o.trail) { const { count = 4, dx = -40, dy = 0, a = 0.22 } = o.trail; for (let i = count; i >= 1; i--) { g.save(); g.globalAlpha *= a * (1 - i / (count + 1)); body(g, { ...o, x: o.x + dx * i, y: o.y + dy * i, trail: null }, p, s, f, of, ink, far, null, alpha(rim?.color || '#fff', 1)); g.restore(); } }
  body(g, o, p, s, f, of, ink, far, rim);
  g.restore();
}
function body(g, o, p, s, f, of, ink, far, rim, flat) {
  const tf = (dx = 0, dy = 0) => { g.translate(o.x + dx, o.y + dy); g.scale(f * s, s); if (o.rot) { g.translate(p.hip[0], p.hip[1]); g.rotate(o.rot); g.translate(-p.hip[0], -p.hip[1]); } };
  if (flat) { g.save(); tf(); parts(g, o, p, { ...of, _sil: flat }, flat, flat, 'sil'); g.restore(); return; }
  if (rim) { g.save(); tf(rim.dir[0] * rim.w, rim.dir[1] * rim.w); parts(g, o, p, { ...of, _sil: rim.color }, rim.color, rim.color, 'sil', 1.6); g.restore(); }
  g.save(); tf(); parts(g, o, p, of, o.dim ? mix(ink, far, 0.6) : ink, far, 'main'); g.restore();
}
function parts(g, o, p, of, ink, far, pass, grow = 0) {
  const sil = pass === 'sil', L = 12.5 + grow, A = 11 + grow, T = 15 + grow, HR = 26 + grow * 0.6;
  const sl = sleeve(of);
  // far leg + far arm
  seg(g, [p.hip, p.kB, p.fB, [p.fB[0] + 12, p.fB[1]]], L - 1, sil ? ink : of.legs ? shade(of.legs, -0.35) : far);
  seg(g, [p.sh, p.eB, p.hB], A - 1, sil ? ink : far);
  if (!sil && sl) seg(g, sl === 'long' ? [p.sh, p.eB, [lerp(p.eB[0], p.hB[0], 0.7), lerp(p.eB[1], p.hB[1], 0.7)]] : [p.sh, [lerp(p.sh[0], p.eB[0], 0.75), lerp(p.sh[1], p.eB[1], 0.75)]], 16, sleeveColor(of, true));
  if (of.gloves) circle(g, p.hB[0], p.hB[1], 8.5 + grow, sil ? ink : shade(of.gloves, -0.2));
  // hair / hood behind head
  if (of.top === 'hoodie' && of.head !== 'hood') { g.fillStyle = sil ? ink : shade(of.color || '#d64a33', -0.28); g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(p.sh[0] - 9, p.sh[1] - 6, 15 + grow, 11 + grow, -0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
  if (of.head === 'hood') { const c = of.headColor || of.color || '#d64a33'; circle(g, p.head[0] - 6, p.head[1] + 2, HR * 1.22, sil ? ink : shade(c, -0.12)); }
  if (['ponytail', 'bun'].includes(of.head)) drawHeadwear(g, of, p.head[0], p.head[1], HR, pass);
  // torso
  seg(g, [p.sh, p.hip], T, ink);
  if (of.top && of.top !== 'none') torsoWear(g, of, p, pass);
  // near leg
  seg(g, [p.hip, p.kF, p.fF, [p.fF[0] + 13, p.fF[1]]], L, sil ? ink : of.legs || ink);
  if (!sil && ['labcoat', 'trench'].includes(of.top)) { const c = of.top === 'labcoat' ? '#ebe7f0' : of.color || '#2c2833'; poly(g, [[p.sh[0] - 4, p.sh[1] + 40], [p.hip[0] + 25, p.hip[1] + 44], [p.hip[0] - 23, p.hip[1] + 44]], c); }
  // near arm
  seg(g, [p.sh, p.eF, p.hF], A, ink);
  if (!sil && sl) seg(g, sl === 'long' ? [p.sh, p.eF, [lerp(p.eF[0], p.hF[0], 0.72), lerp(p.eF[1], p.hF[1], 0.72)]] : [p.sh, [lerp(p.sh[0], p.eF[0], 0.75), lerp(p.sh[1], p.eF[1], 0.75)]], 16.5, sleeveColor(of, false));
  if (of.gloves) circle(g, p.hF[0], p.hF[1], 9 + grow, sil ? ink : of.gloves);
  // head
  circle(g, p.head[0], p.head[1], HR, ink);
  if (!['ponytail', 'bun', 'hood', 'none', undefined].includes(of.head)) drawHeadwear(g, of, p.head[0], p.head[1], HR, pass);
  if (sil) return;
  face(g, p.head[0], p.head[1], HR, { expr: o.expr, talk: o.talk, blink: o.blink, look: o.look, override: o.face });
  if (of.face === 'glasses') { const c = S().feature; g.strokeStyle = c; g.lineWidth = 2.4; for (const dx of [-6.5, 10]) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.head[0] + 9 + dx, p.head[1] - 4, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); } seg(g, [[p.head[0] + 9.5, p.head[1] - 4], [p.head[0] + 12.5, p.head[1] - 4]], 2, c); }
  if (of.face === 'sunglasses') { g.fillStyle = '#05060a'; rr(g, p.head[0] - 1, p.head[1] - 10, 16, 11, 4); g.fill(); rr(g, p.head[0] + 17, p.head[1] - 10, 15, 11, 4); g.fill(); seg(g, [[p.head[0] - 4, p.head[1] - 7], [p.head[0] + 33, p.head[1] - 7]], 2.5, '#05060a'); g.fillStyle = '#9fe8ff'; g.fillRect(p.head[0] + 2, p.head[1] - 8, 4, 2); g.fillRect(p.head[0] + 20, p.head[1] - 8, 4, 2); }
  if (of.head === 'hood') { g.strokeStyle = shade(of.headColor || of.color || '#d64a33', 0.15); g.lineWidth = 5; g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.head[0] - 6, p.head[1] + 2, HR * 1.18, Math.PI * 0.9, Math.PI * 1.75); g.stroke(); }
  if (o.prop) drawProp(g, o.prop, p, ink);
}
export function soccerBall(g, x, y, r, rot = 0, light = '#f2efe8') {
  g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate(rot); circle(g, 0, 0, r, light);
  g.fillStyle = '#16131c'; const pent = (cx, cy, rr2) => { g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * Math.PI * 2 / 5; g.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * rr2, cy + Math.sin(a) * rr2); } g.closePath(); g.fill(); };
  pent(0, 0, r * 0.32); for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * Math.PI * 2 / 5 + Math.PI / 5; g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.clip(); pent(Math.cos(a) * r * 0.85, Math.sin(a) * r * 0.85, r * 0.28); g.restore(); }
  g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(r * 0.15, r * 0.15, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.arc(0, 0, r, 0, Math.PI * 2, true); g.fill(); g.restore();
}
function drawProp(g, prop, p, ink) {
  const t = typeof prop === 'string' ? { type: prop } : prop; const h = t.hand === 'B' ? p.hB : p.hF;
  switch (t.type) {
    case 'phone': rr(g, h[0] - 2, h[1] - 22, 14, 24, 3); g.fillStyle = ink; g.fill(); g.fillStyle = t.glow || '#9fd8ff'; g.fillRect(h[0], h[1] - 20, 10, 18); break;
    case 'ball': soccerBall(g, h[0] + 10, h[1] - 6, 14, t.rot || 0); break;
    case 'paper': g.fillStyle = '#f2e6c8'; g.fillRect(h[0] - 4, h[1] - 30, 30, 36); g.strokeStyle = '#b8a888'; g.lineWidth = 1.5; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(h[0], h[1] - 22 + i * 7); g.lineTo(h[0] + 20, h[1] - 22 + i * 7); g.stroke(); } break;
    case 'cup': g.fillStyle = '#f2efe8'; g.fillRect(h[0] - 2, h[1] - 18, 14, 18); seg(g, [[h[0] + 12, h[1] - 14], [h[0] + 17, h[1] - 9], [h[0] + 12, h[1] - 4]], 2.5, '#f2efe8'); break;
    case 'paddle': circle(g, h[0] + 12, h[1] - 6, 11, t.color || '#b2382a'); break;
    case 'pointer': seg(g, [h, [h[0] + 70, h[1] - 50]], 2.5, '#d8d0c0'); break;
    case 'tablet': rr(g, h[0] - 6, h[1] - 34, 40, 30, 4); g.fillStyle = ink; g.fill(); g.fillStyle = t.glow || '#7fd0d6'; g.fillRect(h[0] - 2, h[1] - 30, 32, 22); break;
    case 'flashlight': seg(g, [h, [h[0] + 14, h[1] - 4]], 6, '#3a3a44'); g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,240,200,.14)'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(h[0] + 14, h[1] - 4); g.lineTo(h[0] + 260, h[1] - 70); g.lineTo(h[0] + 260, h[1] + 60); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.restore(); break;
  }
}
// world position of the head centre (for rings, labels, speech bubbles)
export function headPos(o) { const p = o.pose || POSES.stand, s = (o.s ?? 1) * (o.outfit?.kid ? 0.72 : 1), f = o.facing ?? 1; let [x, y] = p.head; if (o.rot) { const [hx, hy] = p.hip; const c = Math.cos(o.rot), sn = Math.sin(o.rot); [x, y] = [hx + (x - hx) * c - (y - hy) * sn, hy + (x - hx) * sn + (y - hy) * c]; } return [o.x + x * f * s, o.y + y * s, 26 * s]; }

// ---------- giant close-up head (fills the frame) ----------
// bigFace(g, cx, cy, r, { outfit, expr, talk, blink, look, rimColor, rimSide:-1|1, tilt })
export function bigFace(g, cx, cy, r, o = {}) {
  const st = S(), ink = st.ink, of = o.outfit || {}, rc = o.rimColor || '#ffc68a', side = o.rimSide ?? -1;
  g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); if (o.tilt) g.rotate(o.tilt);
  // shoulders / clothing at the bottom
  const top = of.top || 'none', cc = top === 'labcoat' ? '#ebe7f0' : of.color || ink;
  g.fillStyle = top === 'none' ? ink : cc; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(0, r * 1.65, r * 1.25, r * 0.85, 0, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
  if (of.head === 'hood') { const hc = of.headColor || of.color || '#d64a33'; circle(g, side * -r * 0.04, r * 0.06, r * 1.2, shade(hc, -0.15)); g.strokeStyle = alpha(shade(hc, 0.4), 0.7); g.lineWidth = r * 0.05; g.beginPath(); g.arc(-r * 0.05, 0, r * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.5); g.stroke(); }
  // rim crescent on the lit side
  g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, r + r * 0.06, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.arc(side * r * 0.07, r * 0.02, r, 0, Math.PI * 2, true); g.fillStyle = rc; g.fill('evenodd'); g.restore();
  circle(g, 0, 0, r, ink);
  if (of.head && !['hood', 'none'].includes(of.head)) drawHeadwear(g, { ...of, _ink: ink }, 0, 0, r, 'main');
  face(g, 0, 0, r, { expr: o.expr, talk: o.talk, blink: o.blink, look: o.look, front: true, override: o.face });
  if (of.face === 'glasses') { g.strokeStyle = st.feature; g.lineWidth = r * 0.06; for (const dx of [-0.34, 0.34]) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(dx * r, -r * 0.16, r * 0.26, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); } }
  if (of.face === 'sunglasses') { g.fillStyle = '#05060a'; rr(g, -r * 0.66, -r * 0.34, r * 0.6, r * 0.4, r * 0.12); g.fill(); rr(g, r * 0.06, -r * 0.34, r * 0.6, r * 0.4, r * 0.12); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(160,230,255,.6)'; g.fillRect(-r * 0.55, -r * 0.28, r * 0.12, r * 0.05); }
  g.restore();
}

// engine/styles.js — colour moods + switchable visual styles.
// A MOOD is the lighting situation of a location (day, dusk, night, storm, interiors, void).
// A STYLE re-grades every mood (palette overrides + a lift/gamma/gain colour grade on the final frame + post settings).
// grade: contrast (around mid-grey), sat (saturation), lift/gamma/gain per RGB channel (lift tints shadows, gain tints highlights).
// Scenes must take colours from mood()/S() so a style switch re-themes the whole video.

// engine/three-kit.js — low-poly 3D for establishing shots, orbits and fly-throughs (Three.js).
// Same look as the 2D shots: flat colours, black stick figures with a light outline, warm key light,
// real shadows, additive light shafts, bloom. 1 world unit = 1 metre.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { EffectComposer } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
import { RenderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
import { UnrealBloomPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js';
import { OutputPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/OutputPass.js';
import { E, S, mood, shade, rng, key, ease, clamp, lerp } from './core.js';
import { POSES } from './rig2d.js';
export { THREE };

let R = null;
export function getRenderer(canvas) {
  if (R) return R;
  R = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, preserveDrawingBuffer: true, alpha: false });
  R.setPixelRatio(1); R.setSize(Math.round(E.W * E.PX), Math.round(E.H * E.PX), false);
  R.shadowMap.enabled = true; R.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  R.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping; R.toneMappingExposure = 1.0; R.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  return R;
}
const C = (c) => new THREE.Color(c);
export const mat = (c, o = {}) => { const { flat, ...rest } = o; return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: C(c), roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0, flatShading: !!flat, ...rest }); };
export const basic = (c, o = {}) => new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: C(c), ...o });
export function canvasTex(w, h, draw, repeat = null) { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; draw(c.getContext('2d'), w, h); const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 4; if (repeat) { t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.repeat.set(...repeat); } return t; }

// stage({ mood:'dusk', fov:35, bloom, fog:[near,far], sky:[top,bottom] | colour, shadowSize:14 })
export function stage(o = {}) {
  const M = mood(o.mood || 'dusk'), st = S();
  const scene = new THREE.Scene(); const sky = o.sky || M.sky;
  scene.background = Array.isArray(sky) ? canvasTex(4, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); sky.forEach((c, i) => gr.addColorStop(i / (sky.length - 1), c)); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }) : C(sky);
  if (o.fog !== false) scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(C(o.fogColor || M.dark), ...(o.fog || [18, 70]));
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(o.fov || 35, E.W / E.H, 0.1, 500);
  const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(C(o.ambientSky || M.ambient), C(M.dark), o.ambient ?? 0.9); scene.add(hemi);
  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(C(o.keyColor || M.key), o.keyIntensity ?? 2.3); sun.position.set(...(o.keyPos || [-6, 10, -8])); sun.castShadow = true;
  const ss = o.shadowSize || 14; sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -ss, right: ss, top: ss, bottom: -ss, near: 0.5, far: 80 }); sun.shadow.bias = -0.0004; sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.02;
  scene.add(sun, sun.target);
  const w = Math.round(E.W * E.PX), h = Math.round(E.H * E.PX);
  const composer = new EffectComposer(R, new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(w, h, { samples: 4, type: THREE.HalfFloatType }));
  composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));
  const bloom = new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(w, h), o.bloom ?? (st.grade.bloom ?? 0.55) * 0.7, 0.5, o.bloomThreshold ?? 0.88); composer.addPass(bloom); composer.addPass(new OutputPass());
  const api = {
    scene, camera, composer, sun, hemi, M, bloom,
    add: (...objs) => { objs.forEach(ob => scene.add(ob)); return objs[0]; },
    render: () => composer.render(),
    dispose: () => { scene.traverse(x => { x.geometry?.dispose(); [].concat(x.material || []).forEach(m => { m.map?.dispose(); m.dispose(); }); }); composer.renderTarget1?.dispose(); composer.renderTarget2?.dispose(); },
  };
  return api;
}

const prep = (m, cast = true, recv = true) => { m.castShadow = cast; m.receiveShadow = recv; return m; };
export const box = (w, h, d, material, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0) => { const m = prep(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), typeof material === 'string' ? mat(material) : material)); m.position.set(x, y, z); return m; };

export const sphere = (r, material, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0) => { const m = prep(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, 24, 16), typeof material === 'string' ? mat(material) : material)); m.position.set(x, y, z); return m; };
export const plane = (w, h, material, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0) => { const m = prep(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), typeof material === 'string' ? mat(material) : material), false, true); m.position.set(x, y, z); return m; };
export function ground(w, d, color, tex = null) { const m = plane(w, d, mat('#ffffff', { color: C(color), map: tex })); m.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; return m; }
export const planks = (base = '#6a4332', n = 16, rep = [4, 4]) => canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = base; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.28)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let x = 0; x <= w; x += w / n) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, h); g.stroke(); } }, rep);
export const tiles = (base = '#3a3a48', n = 8, rep = [4, 4]) => canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = base; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.25)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo((i * w) / n, 0); g.lineTo((i * w) / n, h); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, (i * h) / n); g.lineTo(w, (i * h) / n); g.stroke(); } }, rep);
// glowing window pane with mullions (emissive, blooms)
export function windowPane(w, h, o = {}) { const { glowColor = '#ffe7b0', frame = '#2b1a12', cols = 4, rows = 5, power = 0.85, lit = true, interior = false } = o; const t = canvasTex(256, Math.round(256 * h / w), (g, W, H) => { if (lit) { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); gr.addColorStop(0, glowColor); gr.addColorStop(1, shade(glowColor, -0.45)); g.fillStyle = gr; } else g.fillStyle = '#1a1c2a'; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); if (interior) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(30,18,14,.75)'; g.fillRect(0, H * 0.6, W, 8); for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++) g.fillRect(14 + k * 34, H * 0.6 - 30 - (k % 3) * 8, 14, 30 + (k % 3) * 8); g.beginPath(); g.arc(W * 0.7, H * 0.9, W * 0.12, 0, 7); g.fill(); } g.fillStyle = frame; for (let i = 0; i <= cols; i++) g.fillRect((i * W) / cols - 5, 0, 10, H); for (let j = 0; j <= rows; j++) g.fillRect(0, (j * H) / rows - 5, W, 10); }); const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: t, color: new THREE.Color(power, power * 0.95, power * 0.85) })); return m; }
// soft additive light shaft from (x,y,z) pointing along dir
let _shaftTex = null;
export function shaft(w, len, color = '#ffcf8a', opacity = 0.05) { _shaftTex ||= canvasTex(64, 256, (g, W, H) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); const gx = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, 0); gx.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); gx.addColorStop(0.2, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gx.addColorStop(0.8, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gx.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'; g.fillStyle = gx; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }); const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, len), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: C(color), map: _shaftTex, transparent: true, opacity, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); m.castShadow = false; return m; }
// building with emissive window grid
export function building(w, h, d, o = {}) { const { color = '#2a2238', win = '#ffcf8a', lit = 0.55, seed = 3 } = o; const r = rng(seed); const cols = Math.max(2, Math.round(w / 1.6)), rows = Math.max(2, Math.round(h / 2.2)); const t = canvasTex(cols * 32, rows * 40, (g, W, H) => { g.fillStyle = color; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { g.fillStyle = r() < lit ? win : shade(color, -0.35); g.fillRect(i * 32 + 8, j * 40 + 10, 16, 22); } }); const front = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, emissiveMap: t, emissive: C('#ffffff'), emissiveIntensity: 0.55, roughness: 1 }); const side = mat(color); const m = prep(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), [side, side, side, side, front, side])); m.position.y = h / 2; return m; }
export function tree3D(x, z, s = 1, color = '#1c1626') { const g = new THREE.Group(); g.add(cyl(0.12 * s, 0.16 * s, 2.2 * s, color, 0, 1.1 * s, 0, 8)); for (const [dx, dy, dz, r] of [[0, 3, 0, 1.1], [-0.6, 2.5, 0.3, 0.8], [0.6, 2.6, -0.2, 0.85]]) g.add(sphere(r * s, mat(color, { flat: true }), dx * s, dy * s, dz * s)); g.position.set(x, 0, z); return g; }
export function pine3D(x, z, s = 1, color = '#1c1626') { const m = cone(0.7 * s, 4 * s, color, x, 2 * s, z, 7); return m; }
// instanced crowd on tiered stands: rows along x, stepping back in z and up in y
export function crowd3D(o = {}) { const { rows = 12, perRow = 80, width = 60, step = 0.8, rise = 0.5, colors = ['#3a3358', '#4a4068', '#2e2a48', '#5a4a70'], seed = 4, z0 = 0, y0 = 0 } = o; const r = rng(seed); const geo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.16, 0.45, 3, 6); const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat('#ffffff'), rows * perRow); const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), col = new THREE.Color(); let i = 0; for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) for (let k = 0; k < perRow; k++) { m4.makeTranslation(-width / 2 + (k + r() * 0.5) * (width / perRow), y0 + j * rise + 0.5, z0 - j * step); mesh.setMatrixAt(i, m4); mesh.setColorAt(i, col.set(colors[Math.floor(r() * colors.length)])); i++; } mesh.castShadow = false; mesh.receiveShadow = true; const steps = new THREE.Group(); for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) steps.add(box(width, 0.3, step, mat(shade(colors[0], -0.4)), 0, y0 + j * rise, z0 - j * step)); const g = new THREE.Group(); g.add(mesh, steps); return g; }

// ---------- 3D stick figure (shares POSES with the 2D rig) ----------
const U = 1 / 140; // 2D rig units -> metres (figure ≈ 1.7 m)
export function figure3D(outfit = {}, o = {}) {
  const st = S(), ink = mat(st.ink, { roughness: 0.75 }), rimC = o.rim || '#ffd39b', out = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: C(rimC), side: THREE.BackSide });
  const root = new THREE.Group(), g = new THREE.Group(); root.add(g); g.scale.setScalar((o.s || 1) * (outfit.kid ? 0.72 : 1));
  const cylGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1, 1, 1, 12), sphGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 16, 12);
  const clothes = outfit.top && outfit.top !== 'none' ? mat(outfit.top === 'labcoat' ? '#ebe7f0' : outfit.color || '#d64a33') : null;
  const limbs = [];
  const mk = (a, b, r, m = ink) => { const core = new THREE.Mesh(cylGeo, m), ol = new THREE.Mesh(cylGeo, out); core.castShadow = true; g.add(ol, core); const j = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, m), jo = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, out); j.castShadow = true; g.add(jo, j); limbs.push({ a, b, r, core, ol, j, jo }); };
  const long = ['hoodie', 'tracksuit', 'labcoat', 'trench', 'suit'].includes(outfit.top);
  mk('sh', 'hip', clothes ? 0.085 : 0.05, clothes || ink);
  mk('hip', 'kF', 0.045); mk('kF', 'fF', 0.042, outfit.legs ? mat(outfit.legs) : ink); mk('hip', 'kB', 0.045); mk('kB', 'fB', 0.042, outfit.legs ? mat(outfit.legs) : ink);
  mk('sh', 'eF', clothes && outfit.top !== 'dress' ? 0.055 : 0.04, clothes && outfit.top !== 'dress' ? clothes : ink); mk('eF', 'hF', long ? 0.05 : 0.038, long ? clothes : ink);
  mk('sh', 'eB', clothes && outfit.top !== 'dress' ? 0.055 : 0.04, clothes && outfit.top !== 'dress' ? clothes : ink); mk('eB', 'hB', long ? 0.05 : 0.038, long ? clothes : ink);
  const head = new THREE.Group(); const hs = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, ink); hs.scale.setScalar(26 * U); hs.castShadow = true; const ho = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, out); ho.scale.setScalar(26 * U * 1.07); head.add(ho, hs);
  const eyeM = basic(st.feature); for (const z of [-0.06, 0.06]) { const e = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, eyeM); e.scale.setScalar(0.022); e.position.set(0.17, 0.02, z); head.add(e); }
  const hc = outfit.headColor || '#f2c21a', hm = mat(hc, { roughness: 0.5 });
  if (['cap', 'capBack', 'visor'].includes(outfit.head)) { const dome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.195, 20, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), hm); dome.position.y = 0.03; const brim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.2, 0.2, 0.015, 20, 1, false, -0.9, 1.8), hm); brim.position.set(outfit.head === 'capBack' ? -0.1 : 0.1, 0.04, 0); if (outfit.head === 'capBack') brim.rotation.y = Math.PI; head.add(dome, brim); }
  if (outfit.head === 'beanie') { const dome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.2, 20, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), hm); dome.scale.y = 1.15; const pom = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, mat(shade(hc, 0.3))); pom.scale.setScalar(0.06); pom.position.y = 0.25; head.add(dome, pom); }
  if (outfit.head === 'fedora') { const fm = mat(hc); const brim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.33, 0.33, 0.02, 24), fm); brim.position.y = 0.1; const crown = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.15, 0.17, 0.17, 20), fm); crown.position.y = 0.19; head.add(brim, crown); }
  if (outfit.head === 'headband') { const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.19, 0.022, 8, 24), hm); band.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; band.position.y = 0.08; head.add(band); }
  if (outfit.head === 'hood') { const hood = new THREE.Mesh(sphGeo, mat(outfit.headColor || outfit.color || '#d64a33')); hood.scale.setScalar(0.23); hood.position.set(-0.04, 0.01, 0); head.add(hood); }
  g.add(head);
  let coat = null; if (['labcoat', 'trench', 'dress'].includes(outfit.top)) { coat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.11, 0.2, 1, 12, 1, true), clothes); coat.material.side = THREE.DoubleSide; coat.castShadow = true; g.add(coat); }
  const P3 = (p, k) => { const near = /F$/.test(k) ? 0.1 : /B$/.test(k) ? -0.1 : 0; return new THREE.Vector3(p[0] * U, -p[1] * U, near); };
  const up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), tmp = new THREE.Vector3();
  root.setPose = (pose) => {
    const p = typeof pose === 'string' ? POSES[pose] : pose;
    for (const L of limbs) { const A = P3(p[L.a], L.a), B = P3(p[L.b], L.b); const len = A.distanceTo(B); tmp.copy(B).sub(A).normalize();
      for (const [m, k] of [[L.core, 1], [L.ol, 1.22]]) { m.position.copy(A).add(B).multiplyScalar(0.5); m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(up, tmp); m.scale.set(L.r * k, len, L.r * k); }
      L.j.position.copy(B); L.j.scale.setScalar(L.r); L.jo.position.copy(B); L.jo.scale.setScalar(L.r * 1.22); }
    const H = P3(p.head, 'head'); head.position.copy(H);
    if (coat) { const S0 = P3(p.sh, 'sh'), Hp = P3(p.hip, 'hip'); const bottom = Hp.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, outfit.top === 'dress' ? -0.3 : -0.42, 0)); coat.position.copy(S0).add(bottom).multiplyScalar(0.5); coat.scale.y = S0.distanceTo(bottom); coat.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(up, tmp.copy(S0).sub(bottom).normalize()); }
    return root;
  };
  root.setPose(o.pose || 'stand'); return root;
}
// camera helpers
export function orbit(camera, t, o = {}) { const { target = [0, 1, 0], radius = 8, height = 3, from = -0.6, to = 0.2, dur = 5, e = ease.inOut } = o; const k = e(clamp(t / dur)); const a = lerp(from, to, k), r = typeof radius === 'number' ? radius : lerp(radius[0], radius[1], k), h = typeof height === 'number' ? height : lerp(height[0], height[1], k); camera.position.set(target[0] + Math.sin(a) * r, h, target[2] + Math.cos(a) * r); camera.lookAt(...target); }
// dolly(camera, t, [[0, {pos:[x,y,z], look:[x,y,z]}], [4, {...}]])
export function dolly(camera, t, keys) { const v = key(t, keys); camera.position.set(...v.pos); camera.lookAt(...v.look); if (v.fov) { camera.fov = v.fov; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); } }

// engine/ui2d.js — on-screen graphics: titles, data cards, counters, callouts, blueprint diagrams, end screen, captions.
import { E, S, V, clamp, lerp, prog, ease, alpha, shade, mix, seg, circle, ring, rr, poly, text, measure, glow, linGrad, FONTS } from './core.js';
import { drawFigure, POSES } from './rig2d.js';

const fadeIO = (t, dur, inT = 0.35, outT = 0.35) => clamp(Math.min(t / inT, (dur - t) / outT));

// Big glowing title: "WHAT IF ONE SECOND / FELT LIKE FOREVER?" (+ optional part label)
export function titleCard(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { lines = ['WHAT IF', 'TITLE GOES HERE?'], sub = null, dur = 4, y = E.H / 2 } = o; const ui = S().ui;
  const a = fadeIO(t, dur, 0.5, 0.5); if (a <= 0) return;
  const s = 1 + 0.025 * t, big = V(96, 92), small = V(54, 52);
  g.save(); g.globalAlpha *= a; g.translate(E.W / 2, y); g.scale(s, s);
  const fit = (str, size) => Math.min(size, (E.W * 0.86) / Math.max(1, measure(g, str, size) / size));
  const sizes = lines.map((ln, i) => fit(ln, i === lines.length - 1 ? big : small));
  const gaps = sizes.slice(1).map((s2, i) => (sizes[i] + s2) * 0.62), totalH = gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  let yy = -totalH / 2 - (sub ? big * 0.45 : 0);
  lines.forEach((ln, i) => { const isBig = i === lines.length - 1; text(g, ln, 0, yy, { size: sizes[i], color: isBig ? ui.gold : ui.cream, glow: 30, glowColor: 'rgba(255,120,40,.85)', spacing: isBig ? 3 : 4 }); yy += gaps[i] ?? sizes[i] * 0.9; });
  if (sub) { g.fillStyle = alpha(ui.cream, 0.55); g.fillRect(-E.W * 0.22, yy - big * 0.32, E.W * 0.44, 3); text(g, sub, 0, yy + big * 0.32, { size: small * 1.05, color: ui.accent, glow: 24, glowColor: 'rgba(255,80,60,.7)', spacing: 3 }); }
  g.restore();
}

// timing / measurement readout e.g. "0.20 s", "NORMAL: 0.25 s"
export function readout(g, o = {}) {
  const { label = null, value = '0.20 s', x = 200, y = E.H - 200, color = S().ui.gold, size = V(46, 52), a = 1, align = 'left' } = o;
  const ls = size * 0.42, lw = label ? measure(g, label, ls, FONTS.ui, 900) + 18 : 0, vw = measure(g, value, size, FONTS.ui, 900), tot = lw + vw;
  let xx = align === 'center' ? x - tot / 2 : align === 'right' ? x - tot : x;
  g.save(); g.globalAlpha *= a;
  if (label) text(g, label, xx, y + size * 0.08, { size: ls, color: alpha(S().ui.cream, 0.8), font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', spacing: 2 });
  text(g, value, xx + lw, y, { size, color, glow: 16, glowColor: alpha(color, 0.6), font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left' }); g.restore();
}
// Callout label with leader line pointing at something
export function callout(g, t, o = {}) { const { at = [E.W / 2, E.H / 2], to = [E.W / 2 + 200, E.H / 2 - 150], str = 'LABEL', color = S().ui.cream, size = V(30, 36), start = 0 } = o; const p = ease.out(prog(t, start, start + 0.5)); if (p <= 0) return; const ex = lerp(at[0], to[0], p), ey = lerp(at[1], to[1], p); circle(g, at[0], at[1], 6, color); seg(g, [at, [ex, ey]], 2, alpha(color, 0.8)); text(g, str, to[0] + (to[0] > at[0] ? 12 : -12), to[1], { size, color, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: to[0] > at[0] ? 'left' : 'right', alpha: clamp((p - 0.6) / 0.4), spacing: 1.5 }); }
// Dimension line with arrows: "30 m"
export function dimension(g, t, o = {}) { const { x1, y1, x2, y2, str = '30 m', color = S().ui.gold, start = 0 } = o; const p = ease.out(prog(t, start, start + 0.6)); if (p <= 0) return; const ex = lerp(x1, x2, p), ey = lerp(y1, y2, p); seg(g, [[x1, y1], [ex, ey]], 3, color); const ang = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1), ar = (x, y, a) => poly(g, [[x, y], [x - Math.cos(a - 0.4) * 16, y - Math.sin(a - 0.4) * 16], [x - Math.cos(a + 0.4) * 16, y - Math.sin(a + 0.4) * 16]], color); ar(x1, y1, ang + Math.PI); if (p > 0.98) ar(x2, y2, ang); text(g, str, (x1 + x2) / 2 + 24, (y1 + y2) / 2, { size: V(40, 46), color, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', glow: 12, glowColor: alpha(color, 0.6), alpha: clamp((p - 0.5) * 2) }); }
// Data card: title, subtitle, animated counter + curve drawing on
export function dataCard(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { title = 'SUBJECT 11 · MAX', subtitle = 'SPEED FACTOR', from = 10, to = 12.4, decimals = 1, unit = 'x', start = 0.4, dur = 2, labels = [[0, null], [0.55, null], [1, null]], shape = 1.7, arrow = true, inDur = 0.35, total = 99 } = o;
  const ui = S().ui; const aIn = ease.out(prog(t, 0, inDur)) * clamp((total - t) / 0.3); if (aIn <= 0) return;

  text(g, subtitle, cx + 72, cy + 136, { size: V(22, 26), color: ui.sub, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', spacing: 2 });

  const p = ease.inOut(prog(t, start, start + dur)), v = lerp(from, to, p);

  text(g, valStr, vx, cy + 108, { size: V(84, 88), color: ui.accent, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'right', glow: 22, glowColor: alpha(ui.accent, 0.5) });
  if (arrow) poly(g, to >= from ? [[vx + 18, cy + 120], [vx + 54, cy + 120], [vx + 36, cy + 88]] : [[vx + 18, cy + 92], [vx + 54, cy + 92], [vx + 36, cy + 124]], ui.accent);
  const x0 = cx + 110, x1 = cx + cw - 110, y0 = cy + ch - 90, y1 = cy + ch * 0.33;
  const pt = (u) => [lerp(x0, x1, u), lerp(y0, y1, Math.pow(u, shape))];
  g.strokeStyle = ui.accent; g.lineWidth = 6; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i <= 120; i++) { const [x, y] = pt((i / 120) * p); i ? g.lineTo(x, y) : g.moveTo(x, y); } g.stroke();
  const [hx, hy] = pt(p); glow(g, hx, hy, 60, ui.accent, 0.8);

export function compareBars(g, t, o = {}) { const { rows = [{ label: 'REAL', v: 0.3 }, { label: 'FELT', v: 0.9 }], x = V(220, 120), y = E.H - V(250, 520), w = V(700, 840), start = 0, color = S().ui.blueprint } = o; rows.forEach((r, i) => { const p = ease.out(prog(t, start + i * 0.3, start + i * 0.3 + 1)); const yy = y + i * 60; text(g, r.label, x, yy, { size: V(24, 30), color: alpha(S().ui.cream, 0.8), font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', spacing: 2 }); rr(g, x + 120, yy - 10, w, 20, 10); g.fillStyle = alpha(color, 0.15); g.fill(); rr(g, x + 120, yy - 10, Math.max(20, w * r.v * p), 20, 10); g.fillStyle = r.color || color; g.fill(); }); }
// Progress / timeline meter (e.g. TIME vs MEMORY strips)
export function meter(g, t, o = {}) { const { x = 200, y = 300, w = E.W - 400, ticks = 40, label = 'TIME', color = S().ui.gold, start = 0, speed = 1 } = o; text(g, label, x, y - 50, { size: V(40, 44), color, align: 'left', glow: 14, glowColor: alpha(color, 0.5) }); rr(g, x, y - 26, w, 52, 10); g.fillStyle = alpha(color, 0.08); g.fill(); for (let i = 0; i <= ticks; i++) { const big = i % 5 === 0, xx = x + 20 + ((w - 40) * i) / ticks, on = (t - start) * speed * ticks > i; seg(g, [[xx, y - (big ? 18 : 9)], [xx, y + (big ? 18 : 9)]], big ? 4 : 2, alpha(color, on ? 1 : 0.35)); } }
// Blueprint style: dark navy backdrop + faint grid; draw your diagram in bpStroke() colour with seg/ring
export function blueprint(g, o = {}) { const { grid = 60, a = 0.07 } = o; const ui = S().ui; g.fillStyle = ui.bpBg; g.fillRect(-E.W, -E.H, E.W * 3, E.H * 3); g.strokeStyle = alpha(ui.blueprint, a); g.lineWidth = 1; for (let x = 0; x <= E.W; x += grid) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, E.H); g.stroke(); } for (let y = 0; y <= E.H; y += grid) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(E.W, y); g.stroke(); } }
export const bpStroke = () => S().ui.blueprint;
// line-art stick figure for blueprint/diagram segments
export function lineFigure(g, o = {}) { const { x, y, s = 1, pose = POSES.stand, color = '#e8f0ff', facing = 1 } = o; const p = pose; g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.scale(s * facing, s); g.strokeStyle = color; g.lineWidth = 5; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.lineJoin = 'round'; const L = (pts) => { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(...pts[0]); pts.slice(1).forEach(q => g.lineTo(...q)); g.stroke(); }; L([p.sh, p.hip]); L([p.hip, p.kF, p.fF]); L([p.hip, p.kB, p.fB]); L([p.sh, p.eF, p.hF]); L([p.sh, p.eB, p.hB]); g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.head[0], p.head[1], 22, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); g.restore(); }
// Speech/comment bubble with dots (used on end screens)
export function bubble(g, x, y, o = {}) { const { w = 120, h = 70, color = '#f4efe6', dots = true, str = null } = o; rr(g, x - w / 2, y - h / 2, w, h, h / 2); g.fillStyle = color; g.fill(); poly(g, [[x - w * 0.25, y + h / 2 - 4], [x - w * 0.38, y + h / 2 + 18], [x - w * 0.08, y + h / 2 - 4]], color); if (dots && !str) for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) circle(g, x + i * w * 0.2, y, h * 0.09, S().ink); if (str) text(g, str, x, y + 2, { size: h * 0.42, color: S().ink, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900 }); }
// End screen: question + comment bubble + subscribe pill + the whole cast lined up
export function endScreen(g, t, o = {}) {
  const { question = 'What would YOU do?', cast = [], moodColors = ['#1b1426', '#2a1a2e', '#4a2a2a'], rim = '#ffb070' } = o; const ui = S().ui;
  g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, 0, 0, E.H, moodColors); g.fillRect(-E.W, -E.H, E.W * 3, E.H * 3);
  glow(g, E.W / 2, E.H * 0.9, E.W * 0.45, '#ff9a5a', 0.3);
  const a1 = ease.out(prog(t, 0.2, 0.8));
  text(g, question, E.W / 2, V(150, 360), { size: V(64, 66), font: FONTS.serif, weight: 900, italic: true, color: ui.cream, alpha: a1, glow: 18, glowColor: 'rgba(255,170,90,.5)' });
  bubble(g, E.W / 2 - V(260, 170), V(300, 560), { w: V(110, 130), h: V(60, 70) });
  const sp = ease.outBack(prog(t, 0.6, 1.1)); g.save(); g.translate(E.W / 2 + V(60, 60), V(300, 560)); g.scale(sp, sp); rr(g, -150, -38, 300, 76, 38); g.fillStyle = '#e0302a'; g.fill(); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-104, 12); g.quadraticCurveTo(-104, -18, -92, -18); g.quadraticCurveTo(-80, -18, -80, 12); g.closePath(); g.fill(); circle(g, -92, 17, 5, '#fff'); text(g, 'Subscribe', 18, 2, { size: 34, color: '#fff', font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900 }); g.restore();
  const n = cast.length, gap = Math.min(V(150, 120), (E.W * 0.85) / Math.max(1, n)), y0 = E.H - V(170, 420);
  cast.forEach((of, i) => { const k = ease.out(prog(t, 0.3 + i * 0.08, 0.7 + i * 0.08)); drawFigure(g, { x: E.W / 2 + (i - (n - 1) / 2) * gap, y: y0 + (1 - k) * 40, s: V(0.9, 1.05), facing: 1, outfit: of, pose: POSES.relaxed, expr: 'smile', alpha: k, rim: { color: rim, dir: [0, 1] } }); });
}
// Word-level captions (great for 9:16). Shows a 3–4 word phrase, current word highlighted.
export function captions(g, globalT, o = {}) {
  const words = E.words; if (!words.length) return; const { y = V(E.H - 150, E.H * 0.79), size = V(58, 74), max = 4, color = '#ffffff', hi = S().ui.gold } = o;
  let i = -1; for (let k = words.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) if (globalT >= words[k].start - 0.05) { if (globalT <= words[k].end + 0.35) i = k; break; } if (i < 0) return;
  const st = Math.floor(i / max) * max, group = words.slice(st, st + max); const strs = group.map(w => w.word.trim().toUpperCase());
  const w0 = strs.map(s => measure(g, s, size)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + size * 0.3 * (strs.length - 1);
  const sz = size * Math.min(1, (E.W * 0.88) / w0);   // shrink long phrases to fit the frame
  const gap = sz * 0.3, widths = strs.map(s => measure(g, s, sz)), total = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + gap * (strs.length - 1);
  let x = E.W / 2 - total / 2; strs.forEach((s, k) => { const cur = st + k === i; const pop = cur ? 1 + 0.08 * ease.out(prog(globalT, group[k].start, group[k].start + 0.12)) : 1; g.save(); g.translate(x + widths[k] / 2, y); g.scale(pop, pop); text(g, s, 0, 0, { size: sz, color: cur ? hi : color, stroke: sz * 0.16, strokeColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,.85)' }); g.restore(); x += widths[k] + gap; });
}

// sets/cafe.js — street café at golden hour. Reusable by any shot: call cafe(g, t, cam, opts) then draw characters at depth 1.
// Value structure: facade mostly in shadow, small warm windows + lamps are the bright accents, sky glows above.
import { E, V, mood, layer, backdrop, rr, seg, circle, poly, linGrad, radGrad, glow, alpha, shade, mix } from '/engine/core.js';
import * as FX from '/engine/fx2d.js';

export const cafeGround = () => E.H * V(0.82, 0.68);
export function cafe(g, t, cam, o = {}) {
  const M = mood(o.mood || 'golden'), GY = cafeGround(), CX = E.W / 2;
  const wall = shade(M.wall, -0.3), wallDark = shade(M.wall, -0.6);
  layer(g, cam, 0.12, (g) => {                                     // sky + low sun + distant city
    backdrop(g, M.sky);
    FX.sun(g, CX + V(620, 300), GY - V(560, 1000), 64, '#fff3dc', M.key, 5);
    FX.clouds(g, { y: GY - V(900, 1500), h: 200, color: '#ffe7c7', a: 0.16, seed: 3 });
    FX.skyline(g, { x: -E.W, y: GY - V(420, 700), w: E.W * 3, minH: 80, maxH: V(260, 420), color: mix(M.sky[1], wallDark, 0.75), windowColor: alpha(M.practical, 0.5), lit: 0.15, seed: 8 });
  });
  layer(g, cam, 0.55, (g) => {                                     // café facade
    const top = GY - V(560, 860), left = CX - 1500, w = 3000;
    g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, top, 0, GY, [shade(wall, 0.06), wall, wallDark]); g.fillRect(left, top, w, GY - top + 4);
    g.fillStyle = shade(wall, -0.4); g.fillRect(left, top - 16, w, 22);
    const ay = GY - V(430, 560), ah = 58;
    for (const i of [-3, -2, -1, 1, 2, 3]) {
      const ww = 210, wh = V(250, 300), wx = CX + i * 300 - ww / 2 + (i > 0 ? 30 : -30), wy = ay + 46;
      FX.windowFrame(g, { x: wx, y: wy, w: ww, h: wh, cols: 2, rows: 1, frame: wallDark, bar: 9 }, (g, x, y, w, h) => {
        g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, y, 0, y + h, [M.practical, shade(M.practical, -0.45), shade(M.practical, -0.7)]); g.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
        g.fillStyle = alpha(wallDark, 0.75); g.fillRect(x, y + h * 0.55, w, 6); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) g.fillRect(x + 12 + k * 32, y + h * 0.55 - 26 - (k % 3) * 6, 12, 26 + (k % 3) * 6);
        circle(g, x + w * 0.5, y + 34, 9, '#fff3d6'); glow(g, x + w * 0.5, y + 34, 70, '#fff3d6', 0.5);
        g.fillStyle = alpha(wallDark, 0.9); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + w * 0.72, y + h - 34, 22, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.fillRect(x + w * 0.72 - 20, y + h - 14, 40, 20);
      });
      glow(g, wx + ww / 2, wy + wh / 2, 230, M.practical, 0.1);
    }
    FX.door(g, { x: CX - 70, y: GY, w: 140, h: V(330, 380), color: wallDark, open: 0.3, light: M.practical });
    for (let x = left, k = 0; x < left + w; x += 56, k++) { g.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#c9b8a4' : '#8f3328'; g.fillRect(x, ay - ah, 56, ah); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + 28, ay, 28, 0, Math.PI); g.fill(); }
    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.35)'; g.fillRect(left, ay - ah, w, 12); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.25)'; g.fillRect(left, ay + 28, w, 30);
    rr(g, CX - 100, ay - ah - 66, 200, 50, 10); g.fillStyle = wallDark; g.fill();
    g.fillStyle = M.practical; g.font = '700 28px Fredoka'; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.fillText('CAFÉ', CX, ay - ah - 40);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const lx = CX + sx * 130, ly = ay + 70; rr(g, lx - 8, ly, 16, 26, 4); g.fillStyle = '#ffe9c0'; g.fill(); glow(g, lx, ly + 12, 120, M.practical, 0.45); }
  });
  layer(g, cam, 0.9, (g) => {                                      // sidewalk
    g.fillStyle = linGrad(g, 0, GY, 0, GY + 400, [shade(M.floor, -0.1), shade(M.dark, -0.2)]); g.fillRect(-E.W, GY, E.W * 3, E.H * 2);
    FX.floorLines(g, GY, GY + 600, 'rgba(0,0,0,.25)', 0.08);
    for (const i of [-3, -2, -1, 1, 2, 3]) FX.pool(g, CX + i * 300 + (i > 0 ? 30 : -30), GY + 30, 200, 34, M.practical, 0.16);
    FX.pool(g, CX, GY + 20, 160, 30, M.practical, 0.25);
  });
  return { GY, CX, M };
}
// outdoor table with optional cup; returns the table-top y
export function cafeTable(g, x, GY, o = {}) {
  const top = GY - 150, c = o.color || '#1e1418';
  seg(g, [[x, top], [x, GY - 6]], 10, c); seg(g, [[x - 50, GY - 4], [x + 50, GY - 4]], 8, c);
  rr(g, x - 130, top - 12, 260, 16, 8); g.fillStyle = c; g.fill(); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,210,150,.35)'; g.fillRect(x - 124, top - 12, 248, 3);
  return { top: top - 12 };
}
export function cup(g, x, y, s = 1, rot = 0) {
  g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate(rot); g.scale(s, s);
  rr(g, -14, -30, 28, 30, 5); g.fillStyle = '#f2ece4'; g.fill(); seg(g, [[14, -24], [22, -18], [14, -10]], 4, '#f2ece4');
  g.fillStyle = '#4a2a1a'; g.fillRect(-12, -30, 24, 5); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)'; g.fillRect(4, -26, 10, 26); g.restore();
}

// sets/lab.js — research lab at dusk: big window with city skyline, bench, wall board (eye -> brain -> muscle), monitors.
import { E, V, mood, layer, backdrop, rr, seg, circle, ring, linGrad, glow, alpha, shade } from '/engine/core.js';

// shots/01_cafe.js — the café sequence: fly catch, the falling cup, the friend's reaction.
import { E, V, registerShot, mood, layer, blurLayer, veil, ease, prog, key, clamp, lerp, shake, talkAmount, seg, circle, ring, rr, glow, alpha, shade, linGrad } from '/engine/core.js';
import { drawFigure, POSES, posesAt, breathe, bigFace, headPos } from '/engine/rig2d.js';
import * as FX from '/engine/fx2d.js';
import * as UI from '/engine/ui2d.js';
import { cafe, cafeGround, cafeTable, cup } from '../sets/cafe.js';
import { MAX, JAKE } from '../cast.js';

const S = () => V(1.6, 1.7);                       // figure scale for medium shots
const seats = () => ({ max: E.W / 2 - V(210, 190), jake: E.W / 2 + V(210, 190) });
function chairAt(g, x, GY, s, facing, color) { g.save(); g.translate(x, 0); g.scale(facing, 1); FX.chair(g, -18 * s, GY, s, color); g.restore(); }
function fly(g, x, y, t, s = 1) {
  for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) circle(g, x + i * 9 * s, y + Math.sin((t - i * 0.03) * 22) * 6 * s, 1.6 * s, 'rgba(20,16,24,.35)');
  circle(g, x, y, 5 * s, '#120e16');
  const wf = Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 90)); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(230,240,255,.55)';
  g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x - 2 * s, y - 6 * s, 3 * s, 7 * s * wf + 1, -0.4, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x + 3 * s, y - 6 * s, 3 * s, 7 * s * wf + 1, 0.4, 0, 7); g.fill();
}
const flyPos = (t) => [E.W + 60 - t * V(760, 520), E.H * V(0.42, 0.5) + Math.sin(t * 5) * 30 + Math.sin(t * 13) * 8];

// two friends at the café table (shared by s001 / s004)
function tableScene(g, t, o) {
  const cam = { z: o.z ?? 1, x: o.x ?? 0, y: o.y ?? 0 };
  const { GY, M } = cafe(g, t, cam, { mood: 'golden' });
  const { max, jake } = seats(), s = S(), rim = { color: M.rim, dir: [0, -1], w: 4 };
  layer(g, cam, 1, (g) => {
    chairAt(g, max, GY, s, 1, '#24181e'); chairAt(g, jake, GY, s, -1, '#24181e');
    const tb = cafeTable(g, E.W / 2, GY);
    if (o.cupOnTable !== false) cup(g, E.W / 2 + 60, tb.top, 1.2);
    drawFigure(g, { x: max, y: GY, s, facing: 1, outfit: MAX, pose: breathe(o.maxPose || POSES.sit, t), expr: o.maxExpr || 'neutral', look: o.maxLook, rim, shadow: false });
    drawFigure(g, { x: jake, y: GY, s, facing: -1, outfit: JAKE, pose: breathe(o.jakePose || POSES.sit, t, 1, 2), expr: o.jakeExpr || 'neutral', prop: o.jakeProp, rim, shadow: false, blink: (t % 3.3) > 3.2 });
    o.extra?.(g, GY);
  });
  FX.dust(g, t, { count: 40, color: '#ffe2bd', a: 0.35 });
  FX.lensStreak(g, E.W / 2, E.H * 0.3, E.W * 1.2, '#ffe0b0', 0.18);
}

// s001 — "A fly buzzes past your face."  wide, slow push, eyes track the fly
registerShot('s001', { draw(g, t) {
  const f = flyPos(t * 1.05 + 0.2); const { max } = seats();
  const hp = [max + 3 * S(), cafeGround() - 180 * S()];
  const look = [clamp((f[0] - hp[0]) / 300, -1, 1), clamp((f[1] - hp[1]) / 300, -1, 1)];
  tableScene(g, t, { z: 1 + 0.08 * ease.inOut(t / 3.4), x: -40 * ease.inOut(t / 3.4), maxLook: look, maxExpr: t > 1.6 ? 'surprised' : 'neutral', jakePose: POSES.phone, jakeProp: 'phone',
    extra: (g) => fly(g, f[0], f[1], t, 1.4) });
} });

// s002 — "You don't even think."  giant face, focus
registerShot('s002', { draw(g, t, info) {
  blurLayer(g, 14, (g) => cafe(g, t, { z: 1.5, x: -150, y: -120 }, { mood: 'golden' })); veil(g, '#120a10', 0.35);
  const M = mood('golden'); const r = V(300, 330) * (1 + 0.05 * t);
  const fx = lerp(E.W * 0.9, E.W * 0.15, ease.inOut(prog(t, 0.1, 1.3)));
  bigFace(g, E.W / 2, E.H * V(0.6, 0.55), r, { outfit: MAX, expr: 'determined', look: [clamp((fx - E.W / 2) / 400, -1, 1), -0.2], rimColor: M.rim, rimSide: -1 });
  blurLayer(g, 6, (g) => fly(g, fx, E.H * 0.4, t, 4));
} });

// s003 — "You just catch it."  the snatch
function hand(g, x, y, s, closed, sleeve, rimC = '#ffc68a') {
  g.save(); g.translate(-5, -5); handShape(g, x, y, s, closed, rimC, rimC); g.restore(); handShape(g, x, y, s, closed, sleeve, '#0e0b12');
}
function handShape(g, x, y, s, closed, sleeve, ink) {
  seg(g, [[x + 40 * s, y + 20 * s], [E.W + 400, y + 260 * s]], 70 * s, ink);
  seg(g, [[x + 150 * s, y + 70 * s], [E.W + 400, y + 260 * s]], 96 * s, sleeve);
  if (closed) { circle(g, x, y, 46 * s, ink); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) circle(g, x - 34 * s + i * 4 * s, y - 30 * s + i * 20 * s, 15 * s, ink); }
  else { circle(g, x + 10 * s, y + 6 * s, 40 * s, ink); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const a = -2.2 + i * 0.32; seg(g, [[x, y], [x + Math.cos(a) * 95 * s, y + Math.sin(a) * 95 * s]], 17 * s, ink); } seg(g, [[x + 6 * s, y + 20 * s], [x - 60 * s, y + 60 * s]], 18 * s, ink); }
}
registerShot('s003', { draw(g, t) {
  const M = mood('golden');
  blurLayer(g, 16, (g) => cafe(g, t, { z: 1.8, x: 200, y: -60 }, { mood: 'golden' })); veil(g, '#120a10', 0.35);
  const snap = 0.5, closed = t > snap, k = ease.out(prog(t, 0, snap));
  const fxy = [E.W * 0.47 + Math.sin(t * 9) * 10, E.H * 0.45 + Math.sin(t * 15) * 8];
  const [sx, sy] = closed ? shake(t, 10 * (1 - prog(t, snap, snap + 0.5)), 30) : [0, 0];
  g.save(); g.translate(sx, sy);
  if (!closed) fly(g, fxy[0], fxy[1], t, 2.2);
  const hx = lerp(E.W * 0.95, fxy[0] + 20, k), hy = lerp(E.H * 0.75, fxy[1] + 10, k);
  hand(g, hx, hy, V(1.4, 1.2), closed, MAX.color);
  g.restore();
  if (closed) {
    FX.rings(g, fxy[0], fxy[1], t - snap, { count: 3, r0: 60, r1: 420, speed: 1.6, color: '#fff3e0', a: 0.6 * (1 - prog(t, snap, snap + 1.2)) });
    FX.speedLines(g, t, { cx: fxy[0], cy: fxy[1], count: 40, a: 0.35 * (1 - prog(t, snap, snap + 0.6)) });
    UI.readout(g, { label: 'REACTION:', value: '0.025 s', x: V(140, 90), y: E.H - V(170, 620), a: ease.out(prog(t, 0.9, 1.2)) });
  }
} });

// s004 — wide: Max holds up the fist, Jake stares
registerShot('s004', { draw(g, t) {
  const fist = { ...POSES.sit, eF: [24, -150], hF: [34, -188] };
  tableScene(g, t, { z: 1.04, x: -20, maxPose: posesAt(t, [[0, POSES.sit], [0.3, fist]]), maxExpr: 'smirk', jakePose: POSES.sitBack, jakeExpr: t > 0.6 ? 'shocked' : 'surprised' });
} });

// s005 — "Your friend knocks his coffee off the table."  close on the table top
registerShot('s005', { draw(g, t) {
  const GY = cafeGround(), tx = E.W / 2, top = GY - 162, cam = { z: V(2.0, 1.8), x: 20, y: top - E.H / 2 + 40 };
  cafe(g, t, cam, { mood: 'golden' });
  const { jake } = seats(), s = S();
  const elbow = key(t, [[0, 0], [0.8, 0], [1.05, 1], [1.6, 0.85]]);
  const push = ease.out(prog(t, 0.95, 2.0));
  const cx = lerp(tx + 60, tx - 175, push), fall = Math.max(0, t - 2.0);

  layer(g, cam, 1, (g) => {

    const jp = { ...POSES.sit, eF: [28 - elbow * 6, -112 - elbow * 18], hF: [50 + elbow * 10, -128 - elbow * 6] };
    drawFigure(g, { x: jake, y: GY, s, facing: -1, outfit: JAKE, pose: jp, expr: t > 1.2 ? 'scared' : 'happy', rim: { color: mood('golden').rim, dir: [0, -1] }, shadow: false });

    if (t > 1.0 && t < 1.4) FX.rings(g, tx + 70, top - 20, t - 1.0, { count: 2, r0: 10, r1: 70, speed: 2.5, color: '#ffffff', a: 0.6 });
  });
} });

// s006 — "The cup hasn't even started to fall"  TIME FREEZE
function coffee(g, x, y, t, seed = 1) {
  g.fillStyle = '#5a321e'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x - 30, y + 30, 44, 18, -0.5, 0, 7); g.fill();

}
registerShot('s006', { draw(g, t) {

// shots/02_orbit3d.js — s008: 3D orbit around the café table (Three.js). init() builds once, draw() animates.
import { E, V, registerShot, mood, ease, prog } from '/engine/core.js';
import { POSES } from '/engine/rig2d.js';
import { MAX, JAKE } from '../cast.js';

registerShot('s008', {
  kind: '3d',
  init(T) {
    const M = mood('golden');
    const st = T.stage({ mood: 'golden', fov: V(34, 52), fog: [14, 45], keyPos: [-8, 9, 6], keyIntensity: 2.8, ambient: 1.3 });
    const { THREE } = T;
    st.add(T.ground(60, 60, M.floor, T.tiles(M.floor, 10, [10, 10])));

    st.add(T.box(30, 7, 0.4, M.wall, 0, 3.5, -4));
    for (const x of [-6, -3, 3, 6]) { const w = T.windowPane(2.2, 2.6, { glowColor: M.practical, cols: 2, rows: 1, power: 0.8, interior: true }); w.position.set(x, 2.1, -3.78); st.add(w); }
    const door = T.windowPane(1.3, 2.6, { glowColor: M.practical, cols: 1, rows: 1, power: 1.05 }); door.position.set(0, 1.3, -3.78); st.add(door);
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) st.add(T.box(1, 0.6, 1.1, i % 2 ? '#efe4d4' : '#b8402f', -15 + i + 0.5, 3.9, -3.3));
    // table, chairs, cup
    st.add(T.cyl(0.55, 0.55, 0.05, '#2a1d22', 0, 0.75, 0, 24), T.cyl(0.05, 0.05, 0.75, '#2a1d22', 0, 0.37, 0), T.cyl(0.3, 0.3, 0.03, '#2a1d22', 0, 0.015, 0));
    const cup = T.cyl(0.05, 0.04, 0.09, '#f2ece4', 0.15, 0.82, 0.05); st.add(cup);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { st.add(T.box(0.45, 0.05, 0.45, '#24181e', sx * 0.85, 0.48, 0), T.box(0.05, 0.6, 0.45, '#24181e', sx * 1.08, 0.78, 0)); for (const lx of [-0.18, 0.18]) for (const lz of [-0.18, 0.18]) st.add(T.box(0.04, 0.48, 0.04, '#24181e', sx * 0.85 + lx, 0.24, lz)); }

// shots/03_explain.js — numbers, title card, the lab, and the blueprint "how it works" diagram.
import { E, V, S, registerShot, mood, layer, blurLayer, backdrop, ease, prog, clamp, lerp, wordTime, seg, circle, ring, rr, poly, glow, alpha, text, FONTS } from '/engine/core.js';
import { drawFigure, POSES, posesAt, breathe } from '/engine/rig2d.js';
import * as FX from '/engine/fx2d.js';
import * as UI from '/engine/ui2d.js';
import { lab } from '../sets/lab.js';
import { MAX, DOC } from '../cast.js';

// shared blueprint comparison: NORMAL vs YOU reaction time bars (s010 shows normal, s011 adds you)
function reactionBoard(g, t, showYou, tYou = 0) {
  UI.blueprint(g); const ui = S().ui, bpc = UI.bpStroke();
  const x0 = V(560, 140), x1 = E.W - V(200, 120), scale = (x1 - x0) / 0.3;   // 0 .. 0.30 s axis
  const rowY = [E.H * V(0.38, 0.4), E.H * V(0.66, 0.6)];
  seg(g, [[x0, rowY[1] + V(110, 140)], [x1, rowY[1] + V(110, 140)]], 2, alpha(bpc, 0.6));
  for (let k = 0; k <= 6; k++) { const x = x0 + k * 0.05 * scale; seg(g, [[x, rowY[1] + V(100, 130)], [x, rowY[1] + V(120, 150)]], 2, alpha(bpc, 0.6)); text(g, (k * 0.05).toFixed(2) + 's', x, rowY[1] + V(150, 180), { size: V(28, 30), color: alpha(bpc, 0.8), font: FONTS.ui, weight: 800 }); }
  const row = (y, label, value, frac, color, who) => {
    if (!E.vertical) UI.lineFigure(g, { x: V(300, 0), y: y + 120, s: 1.05, pose: who === 'you' ? POSES.ready : POSES.stand, color: alpha(color, 0.9) });
    text(g, label, x0, y - 62, { size: V(40, 44), color, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', spacing: 2 });
    rr(g, x0, y - 26, x1 - x0, 52, 26); g.fillStyle = alpha(color, 0.1); g.fill();
    const w = Math.max(52, (x1 - x0) * frac); rr(g, x0, y - 26, w, 52, 26); g.fillStyle = color; g.fill(); glow(g, x0 + w, y, 60, color, 0.5);
    if (frac > 0.02) text(g, value, x0 + w + 28, y + 2, { size: V(58, 60), color, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, align: 'left', glow: 14, glowColor: alpha(color, 0.6), alpha: clamp(frac * 8) });
  };
  const pN = showYou ? 1 : ease.inOut(prog(t, 0.5, 2.2));
  row(rowY[0], 'NORMAL HUMAN', '0.25 s', (0.25 / 0.3) * pN, bpc, 'normal');
  if (showYou) row(rowY[1], 'YOU', '0.025 s', (0.025 / 0.3) * ease.out(prog(t, tYou, tYou + 0.25)), ui.gold, 'you');
}
registerShot('s010', { draw(g, t) { reactionBoard(g, t, false); } });
registerShot('s011', { draw(g, t, info) {
  const tYou = (wordTime('react', info.shot.start) ?? info.shot.start + 0.4) - info.shot.start;
  reactionBoard(g, t, true, tYou);
  UI.badge(g, { str: '10x FASTER', a: ease.out(prog(t, tYou + 0.6, tYou + 1)), size: V(40, 46) });
} });

// s012 — the title card over a sunset city

// s014 — "Your eyes see. Your brain decides. Your muscles move."  blueprint pipeline, synced to the words
function eyeIcon(g, x, y, r, c) { g.strokeStyle = c; g.lineWidth = 5; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x, y, r, r * 0.55, 0, 0, 7); g.stroke(); circle(g, x, y, r * 0.28, c); }
function brainIcon(g, x, y, r, c) { g.strokeStyle = c; g.lineWidth = 5; for (const dx of [-0.32, 0.32]) { g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(x + dx * r, y, r * 0.42, r * 0.6, 0, 0, 7); g.stroke(); } seg(g, [[x - r * 0.5, y - r * 0.1], [x - r * 0.2, y + r * 0.15]], 4, c); seg(g, [[x + r * 0.5, y - r * 0.1], [x + r * 0.2, y + r * 0.15]], 4, c); }
function armIcon(g, x, y, r, c) { g.strokeStyle = c; g.lineWidth = 6; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x - r * 0.7, y + r * 0.4); g.lineTo(x, y + r * 0.4); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + r * 0.6, y + r * 0.3, x + r * 0.3, y - r * 0.5); g.stroke(); circle(g, x + r * 0.3, y - r * 0.55, r * 0.2, c); }
registerShot('s014', { draw(g, t, info) {
  UI.blueprint(g); const c = UI.bpStroke(), gold = S().ui.gold, g0 = info.shot.start;
  const T = ['eyes', 'brain', 'muscles'].map((w, i) => (wordTime(w, g0) ?? g0 + 0.2 + i * 1.3) - g0);
  const pts = E.vertical ? [[E.W / 2, E.H * 0.2], [E.W / 2, E.H * 0.41], [E.W / 2, E.H * 0.62]] : [[E.W * 0.2, E.H * 0.48], [E.W * 0.5, E.H * 0.48], [E.W * 0.8, E.H * 0.48]];
  const icons = [eyeIcon, brainIcon, armIcon], labels = ['SEE', 'DECIDE', 'MOVE'], R = V(90, 110);
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) { const k = ease.inOut(prog(t, T[i] + 0.2, T[i + 1])); const [ax, ay] = pts[i], [bx, by] = pts[i + 1];
    g.setLineDash([10, 12]); seg(g, [[ax + (bx - ax) * 0.18, ay + (by - ay) * 0.18], [bx - (bx - ax) * 0.18, by - (by - ay) * 0.18]], 3, alpha(c, 0.35)); g.setLineDash([]);
    if (k > 0 && k < 1) { const px = lerp(ax, bx, 0.18 + 0.64 * k), py = lerp(ay, by, 0.18 + 0.64 * k); glow(g, px, py, 50, gold, 0.9); circle(g, px, py, 9, '#fff8e0'); } }
  pts.forEach(([x, y], i) => { const on = ease.outBack(prog(t, T[i] - 0.1, T[i] + 0.3)); g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.scale(0.6 + 0.4 * on, 0.6 + 0.4 * on); const col = t > T[i] ? gold : alpha(c, 0.5);
    ring(g, 0, 0, R, 4, col); if (t > T[i]) glow(g, 0, 0, R * 1.6, gold, 0.25); icons[i](g, 0, 0, R * 0.55, col); g.restore();
    text(g, labels[i], x + (E.vertical ? R + 40 : 0), y + (E.vertical ? 0 : R + 60), { size: V(44, 50), color: col, font: FONTS.ui, weight: 900, spacing: 3, align: E.vertical ? 'left' : 'center', alpha: clamp(on) }); });
  const total = T[2] + 0.6, ms = clamp(t / total) * 0.25;
  UI.readout(g, { label: 'ELAPSED', value: ms.toFixed(3) + ' s', x: E.W / 2, y: E.H * V(0.84, 0.71), align: 'center', a: ease.out(prog(t, 0.2, 0.6)) });
} });

# Bible — "What If Your Reaction Time Was 10x Faster?" (47 s excerpt)
Logline: second-person story; "you" (Max) has 10x reaction time. Tone: wonder + light suspense, then explainer.
## Cast
| id | role | outfit | signature | expressions |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MAX | you, hero | hoodie #d64a33, no hat | red hoodie | neutral, surprised, determined, smirk, thinking |
| JAKE | best friend | blue tee, yellow cap #f2c21a | yellow cap | happy, scared, shocked, surprised |
| DOC | scientist | lab coat + round glasses | white coat | smile, talk, point |
## Sets
- cafe (golden): street café facade, striped awning, small warm windows with shelf silhouettes, low sun right, distant skyline; key light = windows behind (rim dir [0,-1]).
- lab (interiorWarm): big window with sunset skyline + blinds, wall board eye->brain->muscle, bench, monitor, clock.
- blueprint (void): navy grid, light-blue line art, gold highlights.
## Motifs
Frozen droplets + cool tint + "100x" badge = time slowed. Gold numbers = Max's speed.
## Music
